# Operator rulings

The operator's decisions, one row each, in the operator's own words where they
were typed and as the chosen option where one was offered. This is the
decision record: an FRD clause, an ADR, a code comment or a test that cites
`Rnnn` means a row here. Rows are appended, never edited; a later ruling that
changes an earlier one is a new row that names it. The register began during
the second audit of 15 October 2026; the rows to R457 came from the
release-readiness programme of 15 to 26 October 2026, were written against the
code of their day and keep their plan and issue references as provenance.

| Id | Question (short) | Answer |

One might have been better, or certainly less. | R550 | Edit leases, 30 October 2026: a second editor on a Case a member of staff holds seizes the lease at once with Take over, and the holder learns of it only when a save is refused | The operator's words: **"When someone is editing a case, and someone else needs to edit, it should send a 'Take Over' request. This would: Send the editing user a popup on the case page with the request, the user (or the AI), and accept / deny."** The second editor, staff or the Automation Actor, **asks**; the holder's open Case page shows the request with Accept and Deny. One request stands per Case: another editor is refused while the holder is being asked, and a request the holder never answered gives way to the next. A request is addressed to the holder asked and ends when the holder changes or finishes; the holder's own save, which ends and re-claims the lease, leaves it standing. A request, denial, withdrawal or silence is telemetry; an accepted hand-over and a forced takeover are history |
| R551 | 30 October 2026: how the holder's page and the requester's page learn of a request and its answer; the bell was offered and refused ("I dont think the notifications bell/channel is good for the primary method here. It needs to be more immediate") | Chosen: **a long poll**: one held request of up to about 25 seconds per editing or waiting tab, woken by an in-process per-Case signal and read from the database at entry, so a request shows within a second and a hidden tab's timer throttling cannot delay it; no server push, no new transport, no ADR |
| R552 | 30 October 2026: what happens when the holder does not answer | Chosen: **Take over anyway after a wait**: the holder has **45 seconds** to answer (chosen over 60 so that one Automation tool call outlasts it under the MCP clients' one-minute default timeout); then the requester's ribbon offers Take over anyway, today's forced, history-recorded takeover, open only to the member of staff who asked and only while the holder asked still holds the lease and has not answered, with **no time cap**; a denial offers nothing further until a new request |
| R553 | 30 October 2026: what Accept does, and how the hand-over is recorded | Chosen: Accept lands any change not yet sent, releases the holder's session and hands the lease to the requester in one transaction; the holder's page redraws read-only with "{requester} is editing"; Deny closes the popup and the holder carries on. The hand-over is Case history under the existing takeover event type with its own sentence (no history index change); the sentence is approved at the PR |
| R554 | 30 October 2026: the Automation Actor as requester and as holder. The operator: **"You are not considering the reverse scenario - AI wants to edit a case but the human has the lease. They need to be able to request too."** | The Automation Actor asks through `pegasus_edit_begin` with `takeOver`: the call raises the request and **waits inside the call** for the answer, returning the lease when accepted and a structured refusal (declined, no answer, already asked) otherwise; **no force for the Automation Actor** ("the AI should not have a 'force=true' functionality"); no new tool. A lease the Automation Actor holds is still taken over by staff at once (ADR-0075), since nobody can be asked |
| R555 | 30 October 2026: how the holder is alerted when the Case tab is not in view ("If we can even make their tab/window flash if not opened that would be preferred") | Chosen: **title flash + browser system notification**: the tab title alternates while the page is hidden, and a desktop notification is raised where permission is granted; permission is asked once, on the first gesture inside edit mode |
| R556 | 30 October 2026: which records get the request flow | Chosen: **the Case lease only**; Image Intake keeps today's immediate Take over, and a GitHub issue records standardising the request flow across record types ("record option 2 as an issue on github"); a Triage Case's scope is unchanged |
| R557 | 30 October 2026: how the holder may dismiss the request popup | Chosen: **Accept or Deny only**: no close control and Escape does nothing; the popup is a decision, not a notice |

Roster in `pegasus_vocabulary_get`, names on reads, or a new staff-list tool; and whether the Sign-off Engineers are listed too | "Roster in vocabulary" and "Yes, include it": `pegasus_vocabulary_get` returns the enabled staff (id and username) and the eligible Sign-off Engineers (id, printed name, qualifications, default). No new tool; read outputs keep their identifiers |

| R597 | Create case Principal, 30 October 2026: the principal code was a plain text box. Engineering §Migrations owns the classification |

The button shouldn't cause that." and "I dont want it to spaz out if someone selects like 3 valuations at a time … if they have two going at once figure out an easy way to resolve a race/conflict." Chosen: **"Multiple stays for separate groups"**; a group reads as the **"Area most of the group's bursts sit in"** (a tie to plan order); touching is **"Discs overlap or touch"** (groups chain); the Nature of Incident sentence **"Keep the list for several areas"**; the Case cell reads the **"Dominant area only"**; stale Cases **"Re-derive on next damage Save"** with no backfill |

Chosen: the settings list **"Every block"** (each block's own On report tick goes); the writing is **"In each item's block"**; "Dont include FAO the court as a box but read only for the rest". The save with the skip beside a row: **"Refuse (Recommended)"**, "Skip impacts can be ticked only while no impact is entered." |

| R651 | a.QDOS26157, 3 November 2026: the report form fixed From as instructions@ although QDOS's Send from is desk@, because a report replied from the mailbox its instruction arrived in (12 October 2026); instructions@ only holds staff forwards (R586). Which mailbox answers a staff forward? | **"instructions is where it was forwarded to. It was received into desk. We should consider the instructions email a special case, where it should only be receiving forwards from collision engineers into the instructions email. Any emails received here should be considered as being received by the original email inbox (in this case, desk)"** and **"Just pretend theres no forward email. Pretend instructions IS desk"**. Chosen with it: the answer leaves the forwarding mailbox as a new message (only instructions@ holds a copy to reply in), subject "Re:" the original's without the forward's "FW:"; when the forwarding mailbox cannot send, staff choose From, the Principal's Send from first; it holds for every reply (report, chaser, Mail Reply and Reply all, Triage reply); Reply all and the report's Cc read the original message's own recipients |
| R652 | PR 1878 review, 3 November 2026: R651 as built treated any Collision Engineers staff forward, in any mailbox, as if the original had arrived directly, so a colleague forwarding a Principal's e-mail from their own address into desk@ was answered to the Principal's original sender as a new message, where before desk@ replied in its own thread to the colleague. Should the rule stay per message, or apply only to messages held in instructions@? | **"Only instructions@"**: "Restrict the exception to messages held in the instructions@ mailbox; other staff forwards reply to the forwarder as before." R651's answer holds only for a staff forward held in instructions@ (`PrincipalMailRoutePolicy.StaffForwardMailboxAddress`); one held in any other mailbox is answered from that mailbox in its own thread, Reply to its own Reply-To or From and Reply all adding its own To and Cc, as before R651. ADR-0068 decision 1's "never by its mailbox" now has this one exception |
| R653 | PR 1878 review, 3 November 2026: Reply all to a staff forward reuses the original's own To and Cc, and the original was almost always sent to one of our mailboxes (desk@, engineers@, info@). Only the mailbox actually sending was dropped, so when the forwarder cannot send and staff pick info@, desk@ lands in To and the reply re-enters intake. Which recipients should Reply all drop? | **"Drop sending and forwarding mailbox"**: "Excludes the mailbox the send leaves from and the mailbox that forwarded the message." Reply all to a staff forward held in instructions@ copies neither; the report's Cc read from the forwarded header (R651) drops the forwarding mailbox too |
| R654 | PR 1878 review, 3 November 2026: the amended FRD-21 and R651 say that when the forwarding mailbox cannot send, every reply's From select starts on the Principal's Send from. The report form does this, but the Send chaser page preselected nothing. Which should change? | **"Chaser preselects Send from"**: "Implement the preselect on the chaser page so code matches FRD-21 and R651." The Send chaser's From select starts on the Principal's Send from whenever it has no fixed sender |
| R655 | The operator's question of 4 November 2026, after Release 138: every send's From was fixed to the mailbox the message arrived in (12 October 2026) or, for a staff forward held in instructions@, the mailbox that forwarded it (R651, R652). Four options were put: which sends get a free From, what it starts on, whether Forward changes, and whether the MCP send tools follow | **"Can the sending email now be freely changed? The SOP/autopopulate should be changeable not enforced"**. Chosen: **"Every send"**, **"Today's rule as the default"**, **"Leave Forward fixed"** and **"Yes, same rule as staff"**. From is a default on every send (report, chaser, Mail Reply and Reply all, Triage reply and the MCP send tools), changeable to any send-ready Outgoing mailbox: it starts on the mailbox the message arrived in, for a staff forward held in instructions@ the mailbox that forwarded it, else the Principal's Send from. A reply from another mailbox than the one holding the message leaves as a new "Re:" message to the same recipients, still recorded as the reply to the original. Forward stays with the holding mailbox. MCP `fromMailbox` is accepted on every send but forward; omitted, the same default applies |
| R656 | The operator's request of 4 November 2026: a temporary Administration setting that appends `-p` to every Case/PO Pegasus mints, with every existing Case rewritten and the setting shipped on. Four questions were put: whether existing Cases' Box folders are renamed (Pegasus proves a Case folder by its name), whether one-off registration Cases are included, which Administration page holds the switch, and its label | **"Plan out a temporary addition that is configurable/toggled via administration settings. This would have the following effect: All case/PO numbers generated in Pegasus, when the setting is enabled, are given -p at the end. This will also require any existing cases to have their case/PO updated (ship the setting enabled)"**, with the examples "a.QDOS26157 ... will become: a.QDOS26157-p", a new Audit "a.QDOS26158-p" when enabled and "a.QDOS26158" when disabled, and **"This is intended as a temp setting to aid not having reference numbers collide with old case numbers"**. Chosen: **"Rename folders"**, **"Yes, include them"**, **"Configuration"** and the label **"Add -p to new Case/PO"**. While on, every minted Case/PO ends in `-p` after its prefix and number or registration (`t.QDOS26158-p`, `AB12CDE.2-p`); switched off, new Case/POs mint without it and Cases that carry it keep it. Every existing Case/PO and Audit reference took it once, the Worker renaming their Box folders; history, issued reports and sent mail keep what they recorded. FRD-01's "never changes" has this one exception |
| R657 | CI runners (3 November 2026): R504's premise, "Windows runners cost twice the Linux rate", does not hold for a public repository, where standard runners are free; is the measured run still wanted, with "green and not slower" as the test? Recommended: yes | "questions are the recommended answers" — the measurement stands: `unit`, `infrastructure` and the six `sql-integration` shards run once on `ubuntu-latest`, the shards against the pinned SQL Server container through `PEGASUS_TEST_SQL_*`; each group is adopted only if green and no slower than its Windows figure |
| R658 | CI runners (3 November 2026): should `invariants` move to Linux and so make the documentation link check case-sensitive? Recommended: no | "questions are the recommended answers" — `invariants` stays on `windows-latest`; the link check keeps its case-insensitive resolution |
| R659 | Audatex import (4 November 2026): an AI-generated Audatex-layout estimate (CE001302 VBR040CH/2) prints a work-unit value and its `*` mark as one word (`3.0*`), where a genuine Audatex report prints the `*` apart; the parser cannot read `3.0*` and refuses the whole import. Where should the fix go? Recommended: the parser | **"Parser accepts 'N\*'"**: a line value with a trailing `*` reads as the value; section totals still have to reconcile; the generator is not changed |
| R660 | Report images (4 November 2026): the Overview printed on page 1 only (operator, 7 October 2026). Should it also print on the image pages; where among them; and does Full page apply to that copy? | "Overview image needs to appear on subsequent pages as well as its standard slot as overview image - e.g. if a case has only 1 image, and that image is marked as overview, it appears twice". Chosen: **"First, before Close-up"** and **"Yes, on image pages"**. The Overview prints in page 1's slot and again first on the image pages, then the Close-up and the supporting images in order; Full page applies to its image-page copy, in the report and the images document, and never to page 1's slot |
| R661 | #1801, 4 November 2026: the 92 proof-checklist rows owed from Releases 81–130 (many on since-wiped Cases or behaviour a later release replaced); production carries live Cases since 2 November 2026. How are they resolved? | "Strike all, close #1801" (option: everyday office use on the live estate is the proof for these rows; #1801 is closed, and a defect found later is filed as its own issue) |
| R662 | #1830, 4 November 2026: R585 is worded about Tractable, but Core names no Tractable sender or Principal; its PDF is recognised by layout as a vehicle-capture document (signature `tractable-capture/1`, today the only capture profile). Does the no-new-Image-Intake rule key on the vehicle-capture document role or on Tractable's sender? | Chosen: **"Vehicle-capture document role"**: any designated capture document's photographs are images for an existing Case; with no Case holding the printed registration nothing registers, for the e-mail or a staff forward of it; no vendor or sender name enters Core (FRD-02 EXT-17 already treats the PDF as a document type, not a party) |
| R663 | Same, 4 November 2026: with the automatic registration stopped, a capture e-mail whose registration no Case holds waits in No Case Found with its photographs; today staff can still press Register images on an unlinked, image-only, uncategorised message. Does that stay available on these e-mails? | Chosen: **"Keep it"**: only the automatic registration stops; Register images and Link to case stay staff's manual outs and the classifier agent may set Pre-Pegasus case; the receipt stays image-only and the printed-registration pairing with an existing Case is untouched |
| R664 | The operator's request of 4 November 2026: every regeneration of a report, valuation or fee note was filed in Box as a separate file, filling the Case folder. Should a regeneration replace the earlier file as a Box version instead, for which documents, within which version limit, with what done about files already there, and under which name? | **"Replace as a Box version"**: "When a report is generated, this is stored to box. If that report is re-generated, this would *replace* the report (box shows this as v2, v3, v4 etc, meaning the old version can still be viewed)"; "this also counts for glass's repair estimates, valuation documents etc". Chosen options: reports, fee notes and image packs; the Repair Spec per estimate; Glass's and Brego valuation PDFs (one file per source and registration); Glass's repair estimate files (one file per estimate). **"Accept Box's limit"**: earlier versions are kept as Box versions, within the plan's own version limit. **"Leave them"**: files already in Box stay as they are. **"Keep the first name"**: a new version keeps the first file's Box name |
| R665 | v48 Stage 1 (4 November 2026): the operator's brief, **"Plan a mockup incorporating the following: 1. Send report moves to a dialogue box with a composer - Files clickable to preview - Files renameable. 2. Additional box on the right hand rail for Inspection+Audit cases (only for the audit case). Must show: Original report repair cost; Salvage value (if total loss); Engineers Value (with guide chosen for original report in brackets. E.g. £5000 (Cazana)"**. Stage 2 (4 November 2026): do the mockup's defaults stand as the settlement of items A–O (Send report from the head and the Next action step while editing; the 880px composer with Files below the recipients; Subject editable and prefilled; Files rows with the filed estimates listed; every generation document renameable; the viewer over the dialog; the inline form and the greyed read cells removed; the Original report card after Figures headed "Original report", pence and the guide word in brackets, no new breakpoint; a refused send reopens the dialog and the Already sent line sits under the preview card; every To/Cc box after the first To removable)? | **"Yes, as drawn (Recommended)"**: every lettered item takes the mockup's default; the Send report dialog and the Original report card are built as `pegasus_v48.html` draws them in Proposal mode ([v48 round](https://github.com/collisionengineers/pegasus-design-archive/tree/design/v48-send-dialog-original-report/v48_planning)) |
| R666 | v48 planning (4 November 2026): the Original report card's source: the Inspection report as generated (the frozen snapshot of the newest Inspection generation), or the Inspection work's live values, which the Inspection view can still edit after the report was sent? | **"Inspection report as generated (Recommended)"**: the card reads the newest Inspection generation's snapshot (its printed repair cost, its salvage value when it was a Total loss, its Engineer's Value with the basis card's guide) and draws the absent glyph until one exists |
| R667 | v48 planning (4 November 2026): the report's subject is derived ("Re:" and the instruction's subject, else "{REG} Report") and never staff-edited, while the Triage and Inbox composers show an editable Subject. Editable, prefilled? | **"Editable, prefilled (Recommended)"**: the Send report dialog's Subject is prefilled with the derived subject and staff may change it; what they submit is sent, a blank field sending the derived subject |
| R668 | v48 planning (4 November 2026): Send report exists only in the page-wide edit session today; should the dialog open from read mode too? | **"Only while editing, as today (Recommended)"**: the section head's Send report and the aside's Send report step open the dialog only while the Case is in the viewer's edit session with the report stored; reading offers neither |
| R669 | v48 build (4 November 2026): Add To recipient and Add Cc recipient grew the To and Cc stacks with no way back | **"extra recepients/cc box isn't removable - adjust"**: every To or Cc box after the first To carries its × and can be removed; Add To recipient and Add Cc recipient add a removable box; the one remaining To box keeps none, since a send needs a To |
| R670 | v48 Stage 2 (4 November 2026): the Subject field's maximum length: the Triage composer's 500, or the mail command's 998? | **"500, as the Triage composer (Recommended)"**: one bound for every staff-typed subject; the Core command keeps its 998 ceiling beneath it |
| R671 | v48 Stage 2 (4 November 2026): the mockup drew Rename on every Files row, the Principal's filed estimates included, but a filed estimate row posts nothing. Should filed estimates be renameable in the send? | **"No, generation documents only (Recommended)"**: Rename on the report, fee note, Repair Spec and images of each work; a filed estimate row shows its filed name with no Rename |
| R672 | v48 Stage 2 (4 November 2026): should a renamed file's name be remembered for the next send of the same report, or does each send start from the policy name again? | **"Each send starts fresh (Recommended)"**: a rename applies to that send only; the next send prefills the policy name (the Principal's pattern or "{Case/PO} {REG} {Outcome} report" with its resend dots); no new stored state |
| R673 | The operator's request of 4 November 2026: the Work Centre's Reports sent counted each Case once, so an Inspection + Audit Case's Audit report never counted, and Management Reports counted every Generate as a report produced and every re-send as a report sent. Three questions were put: does a re-send count as another report sent, what happens to Reports produced, and how does the Case list show an Inspection + Audit Case's two reports | **"An inspection+audit type should count as 2 reports"**, "this matters for: reports sent (even if its 1 email, if we're sending 2 reports for that case type, so the work centre stats need to be right)" and "We might generate the report a bunch of times, and not send it. Sending it is the main thing we want to track basically. So our management reports need to track the total sent reports, plus we arent really counting/tracking the audit reports here as two it needs to be clearer on the spreadsheets". Chosen: **"count it seperately as a resend"**, **"Remove it (Recommended)"** and **"One row per report"**. Each report, the Inspection's or the Audit's, counts once in Reports sent, at its first observed Pegasus send, one e-mail carrying both being a send of each; every later send of it is a Re-send, counted on its own; the Work Centre and Management Reports agree. Every produced figure goes: Outcomes, agreed fees and Time to send follow each report's first send, and its fee is the one frozen in the report that send carried. The Case list is one row per report. Approved with the plan: the Work Centre shows no Re-send figure; an Inspection + Audit Case's Audit row is listed, blank, before Create audit; Repair cost and Sign-off engineer stay the latest confirmed report's |
| R674 | Closing a Glass's session (3 November 2026): "Its looking too close to something akin to T&Cs rather than closing a session - redo the view/design on this completely" | As recommended and chosen: the session line keeps a small Close session button that opens the compact dialog ("Dialog from line"); no checkbox, the press is the confirmation that Glass's is closed and no estimate is open ("Press is the confirmation"); the title "Close Glass's session" and the prompt "Only close this once Glass's is closed and no estimate is open. The Glass's account is then free for another estimate." ("Proposed wording") |
| R675 | Test instance, 3 November 2026, question 1: R626 made the instance's mail read-only, and the operator reopened it | **"Alter the process on this - graph parity/subscription re-use from the live deploy is now in scope. This means send perms as well as read perms."**: the instance composes Production's Graph mail side (read, send, the answered-instruction move, change subscriptions) over its own app registration; R626's read-only grant is superseded |
| R676 | Same, question 2: Graph delivers change notifications only to a public HTTPS URL, the instance's origin is tailnet-only, and production's subscriptions are bound to production's URL so they cannot be re-used | Chosen: **"Funnel the webhook path only"**: `tailscale funnel` on port 8443 for `/hooks/microsoft-graph/mail` alone; port 443 stays tailnet-only through `tailscale serve`; the instance creates its own subscriptions under its own client state |
| R677 | Same, question 3: which mailboxes the instance may send from, a send being a real e-mail and the tidy a real move in the shared mailbox; and whether the FRD-21 and FRD-26 acceptance lines change | Chosen: **"Same production mailboxes, full parity"**: Incoming and Outgoing on the mailboxes production uses; the Exchange grant is `Application Mail.Read`, `Mail.ReadWrite` and `Mail.Send` on the scope. **"Leave FRD-21/26 as they are"**: production stays the proof of record; the instance rehearses |
| R678 | Same, question 4: what the production copy does with mailbox state now that the instance writes | Chosen: **"Deactivate and clear subscriptions"**: R627's deactivation stands and the copy deletes every `ApprovedMailboxSubscriptions` row, so a mailbox re-enabled on the instance starts a new generation and a fresh subscription under the instance's webhook |
| R679 | Same, question 5: how the Principal API and the Automation MCP run on the instance, a production copy carrying real Principal key hashes | Chosen: **"Both on, tailnet-only, copied keys paused"**: `Features__PrincipalApi=true` and `Features__AutomationMcp=true` with development keys and the instance's own client secret; the copy pauses every Active Principal API credential; no connector redirect, so the MCP serves the local classifier and tailnet agents |
| R680 | Same, question 6: the answered-instruction tidy does not check activation, so a fresh copy would move production's pending answered instructions | Chosen: **"Yes, close copied pending tidies on import"**: the copy marks every copied Sent report's pending instruction move `Failed` with code `production_copy` |
| R681 | Branch process, 3 November 2026: the operator's rule for `main`, `dev` and every other branch | **"Main branch = always reflective of the exact live deployment. This code is what gets deployed. dev branch = what the test server is ran from. This is vetted and then merged to main. Any additional branches are always to be created from dev. This process allows us to vet and view changes prior to releasing to the live deploy without actually affecting it"** |
| R682 | Same, question 1: the release pushed the approved SHA to `main` before any Azure write (R567), so a failed release left `main` ahead of production | Chosen: **"Promote after the smoke"**: `main` moves only once the Web App, Worker and classifier read back the new SHA and the full smoke passes; a failed release leaves `main` at the live SHA; the promotion-only route (nothing deployable) is unchanged |
| R683 | Same, question 2: CI ran on pull requests and pushes to `main`, never on pushes to `dev`, the tree the instance runs | Chosen: **"Full CI on dev, guard only on main"**: a merge into `dev` runs the full lanes on the merged tree, and the push to `main` keeps the history guard alone; same number of full runs as before |
| R684 | Same, question 3: what "vetted on the test server" means at release time | Chosen: **"Preflight checks the instance's SHA"**: the read-only run reads the instance's `/diagnostics/version` over the tailnet and refuses a candidate the instance is not running; the approval message stays the acceptance |
| R685 | Same, question 4: R629 stacked task/test-instance (#1865) on task/cutover-governance (#1844), and the rule says every branch comes from `dev` | Chosen: **"Land the stack, stacking stays allowed"**: #1865 merges into task/cutover-governance and #1844 into `dev`, then the instance moves to `dev`'s tip; a dependent change may still branch from an open task branch and target it |
| R686 | Semantic Versioning, 3 November 2026: "We are looking to use Semver for our subsequent changes based on this new dev process. This will take effect for all releases/versions after the associated PRs are merged/approved." Question 1: which version the first Semver release carries, and whether the "Release N" counter (last used: Release 136) continues | Chosen: **"1.0.0, version replaces the counter"**: the next release is 1.0.0; the operations heading, the retained artifact folder and the owed-issue titles use the version ("Release 1.0.0"); `-ReleaseNumber` retires and `-Version` is the approved run's input; one identity per release |
| R687 | Same, question 2: who decides whether a release is a MAJOR, MINOR or PATCH bump | Chosen: **"You name it in the approval; the preflight proposes"**: the read-only run prints the live version and a proposed next one, PATCH when every PR in the range closes only `type:bug` issues, MAJOR when the route is destructive (a non-additive migration), MINOR otherwise; the approval message names the version it grants, and the script refuses one that is not a single step above the live version |
| R688 | Same, question 3: whether the release tags `main` and publishes the artifacts, nothing being tagged and the rollback packages existing only on the one release workstation (#1849) | Chosen: **"Tag only"**: the annotated tag `v<version>` goes with the promotion push; no GitHub Release; #1849 stays open for another home |
| R689 | Same, question 4: whether the rule that every branch comes from `dev` settles the hotfix question in #1851, a hotfix branch from `main` breaking the main-contained-in-dev guard | Chosen: **"No hotfix route: a fix ships as a PATCH of dev's tip"**: a fix is vetted on the instance like any change; #1851 keeps only its window and staff-notice questions |
| R690 | Cutover review round 2, 3 November 2026 (#1851): whether the normal release route has a window and a staff notice, every release restarting the Web App for staff (2 to 3 minutes, up to 11 minutes to readiness once) | Chosen: **"No window, no notice"**: a normal release runs when its candidate has passed on the test instance; the restart is the outage; the destructive route keeps its approved window (R605) |
| R691 | Same (#1848): whether `main` and `dev` require the CI checks and refuse direct pushes, the release pushing `main` and the `v*` tag itself | Chosen: **"Rulesets on both, release bypass"**: `dev` requires a pull request and a green `repository-check`; `main` refuses every push except the release's leased push from the operator's account (repository-admin bypass); the waiver sentence leaves the release skill, a non-green pull request waits |
| R692 | Same (#1849): where a rollback's artifacts come from, the retained folders existing only on the one release workstation and every released SHA carrying `v<version>` (R688) | Chosen: **"Rebuild from the tag"**: a rollback uses the retained folder when the workstation holds it, else the previous version is rebuilt from its tag; nothing is copied off the workstation |
| R693 | Test instance mail, 4 November 2026: which mailbox the hosted test instance reads from and sends from, the registration's scope having admitted only the four production mailboxes (R675, R677) | **"mailbox: pegasustest@collisionengineers.co.uk - new shared mailbox created - this is exclusively for the test environment not the live deploy - full send/read perms to be added/obtained"**: the instance's own mailbox is `pegasustest@collisionengineers.co.uk`, never approved on production; `CollisionSpike-Intake-Prod` was widened to it and the instance approved it Incoming and Outgoing on 4 November 2026; a production mailbox is enabled on the instance only when the operator names it and for how long (R675, R677) |
| R694 | Review of the release-readiness PR (#1879), 4 November 2026: the test-instance skill told a reader to clone the repository on any machine, while ADR-0088 has one instance and the operator's WSL box is running it | **"THIS machine is the machine that is running the wsl instance (it might even be running now). if the skill gets ran elsewhere (ie not on windows - any other linux machine is fine) then it should stop."**: the host is the Tailscale node that owns `https://cemattypc-1.tail432b53.ts.net/`, held in `scripts/PegasusPlatform.ps1`; `Start -PublicOrigin`, a restart of the run with its recorded origin and the production copy refuse any other machine, and the skill stops there; moving the instance is an operator decision |
| R695 | Same: R681 has the instance run `dev` and nothing else | **"we should be able to swap the test instance to another branch or PR if needed though (make the necessary changes for this now)"**: the instance runs `dev` by default and a branch or pull request head when the operator names it; the release preflight still refuses a candidate the instance is not serving (R684), so it is back on `dev`'s tip before a release; a head that adds a migration is served only when the operator accepts that the return to `dev` waits for it to land or needs a Reset |
| R696 | Same: whether the instance's data (staff UAT Cases, `pegasustest@`'s mail, a production copy) is disposable as the pre-cutover estate's was | **"This PR is intended to be implementing full release ready protocols into pegasus. this means we cant just treat data as throwaway or test data just because its on pegasus. we have to be more careful, because people are using it."**: `Reset` of the instance's run, or of any run holding a production copy, needs the operator's word for that run (`-Approve`) and refuses without it; the production extract is removed once published and no file of production's data is kept; the copy runs only into the instance's run on its host |
| R697 | The operator's request of 5 November 2026 to remove three live Cases, `QCL26005-p`, `AB12CDE-p` and `QDOS26158-p`, against FRD-01's "A Case is never deleted". Four questions were put: the Box case folders, the Outlook copies of the mail, winding back reference counters, and the maintenance window; a fifth asked how re-ingestion of the retained mail is prevented | **"Perform a targeted removal on the live estate/database. All data generated from/as a result of the following three cases"**, which **"should not show: In any list of cases; In any reports sent stats, any received stats, any management reports; The emails from the cases should be removed"**, and **"These three are test cases that are to be pruned carefully from the live setup as though they were never there."** Chosen: **"Delete"** the Box case folders (to Box trash), **"Pegasus copies only"** (Outlook untouched), **"Leave counters as they are"** (no reference is reused), **"Stop Worker briefly now"**, and **"Advance the two boundaries"** (instructions@ inbox and desk@ Sent, to just past the last test message). A one-off exception for these three Cases; FRD-01 is otherwise unchanged and no product path deletes a Case |
| R698 | QCL26005-p (ME17EBA, a 2017 Toyota Prius), 5 November 2026: VES answered no type approval, a "2 AXLE RIGID BODY" wheelplan and a 1,635 kg revenue weight, and the vehicle-type rule's rigid-body revenue-weight step called it a van; DVLA records a revenue weight for cars as well as vans | Chosen option "Leave type empty": with no type approval and no two- or three-wheel, articulated or multi-axle wheelplan, the Vehicle type is left Not recorded for staff to pick; the revenue-weight step is removed, and a later complete answer that no longer classifies the vehicle withdraws a type the lookup itself recorded |
| R699 | Same Case: DVSA's latest MOT read 62,801 miles on 5 October 2026, but the history held one clean interval, so the estimator answered a qualified range of 62,801–72,101 and, under R290, the Case mileage stayed empty | **"it was a month ago. it should basically be the same. 10,000 mile range is unrealistic. we should be estimating/calculating if we get an MOT value, period."**, then the chosen option "Project at available rate": any usable MOT reading gives the Case a figure. One clean interval is enough for a rate and the forecast has no horizon; with no clean interval the latest reading stands. This replaces R290's range for such histories |
| R700 | Same: whether a history whose readings disagree also always gives a figure — two different readings on one MOT date, or a later reading below the odometer that no test confirms | Chosen option "Keep these as empty": such a history still fills no Case mileage and staff decide |
| R701 | Same: Glass's held no type number for ME17EBA; the placeholder launch's first live return stalled with `glass.export.none` though the Engineer chose a usable Prius, and MVA's search tree found the car, its valuation and a real-vehicle estimate | Chosen option "Record an issue": one GitHub issue holds the placeholder-return evidence and the search-tree route; no code this round |
| R702 | pegasus_report_send (5 November 2026): an AI sent GY13GYE's report with `attach: []` to leave the fee note off, and Core treated the empty list as no choice, so every confirmed document, the fee note included, went. What should an empty attach do? | **"Report alone (Recommended)"**: fix it in Core; leaving attach out still means every confirmed document; any list, an empty one included, means the report plus exactly those documents, with any companions the Principal requires; applies to otherAttach and the staff page too |
| R703 | pegasus_report_send (5 November 2026): the result returned only the send's state, so the AI could not check what the e-mail carried before reporting it. Should the result list the files sent? | **"Yes, list file names (Recommended)"**: the result gains `attachments`, the file names the e-mail carries, in order, the other report's and the filed estimates included |
| R704 | pegasus_report_send (5 November 2026): the schema lists the sent files in `attachments` but never tells the caller to check them before saying what was sent, the check whose absence let the wrong "fee note left off" claim through. Add that instruction? | **"gap1"**: the tool and its `attachments` field say to report what was sent from `attachments`, not from what was asked for |
| R705 | Case QDOS26162-p (5 November 2026): its Inspection report was sent and linked, but under the 13 October 2026 rule the Inspection + Audit Case stayed With Engineer with no Audit. The operator: "We need to reevaluate the exact setup for inspection+audit cases, as these may not necessarily require an audit to be created" and "They might require it though so the option must be present". What does sending the Inspection report do on an Inspection + Audit Case with no Audit? | **"Move to Report sent (Recommended)"**: the send ends the work as on an Inspection; Create audit stays live on Report sent and returns the Case to With Engineer for the Audit. Replaces the 13 October 2026 With Engineer window and the query window it held |
| R706 | Same: once the report of an Inspection + Audit Case with no Audit is stored, its Next action is Create audit only. What should it be? | **"the two reports get sent together, but in this particular inspection+audit case we dont want an audit so this needs planning around"**; asked then how a no-Audit Case is handled on screen: **"Offer both in Next action"**: until the report is sent the Next action draws Create audit and Send report beside it, both with their existing labels |
| R707 | Same: Cases already held With Engineer with their Inspection report's Sent evidence and no Audit (QDOS26162-p in production) | **"Data migration to Report sent (Recommended)"**: a forward-only, data-only migration moves each to Report sent, entered when its evidence was linked; the link's existing event records the send, so no history row is added |
| R708 | Test instance (5 November 2026): Administration > Accounts failed on the instance with "The key {033de326…} was not found in the key ring": the 3 November production copy carried two Glass's logins protected under production's key ring, which the instance does not hold. How should a copy treat them? | Approved plan: **a production copy clears the copied per-user external credentials; production's key ring stays in production, and logins are re-entered on the instance**. The copy script clears them as Clear does and records `externalCredentialsCleared`; run `83ec377d`'s two rows were cleared the same way |
| R709 | Test instance (5 November 2026): the instance had no Glass's, Cazana or Brego guide valuation; the Web composed them under the Production profile only, and R625's four integrations predate ADR-0079 and ADR-0084. Should the instance have them, and on which accounts? | **"yes, all three on the instance with production's accounts"**: `Features:LiveGuideValuation` composes all three on the instance (with `Features:LiveGlass`), and `New-LocalLiveSettings.ps1` reads the six account secrets from `pegasusprodkv252ow37g` |
| R710 | Case Valuation (5 November 2026): ticking Previous total loss starts at −10 %. Which percentage should it start at? | **"When we press the checkbox, it defaults to 10%. We want it to default to 20%. Thats all. No other changes."**: ticking starts at −20 %; −10 % stays on offer |
| R711 | Slowness investigation (5 November 2026): production Web runs on a B1 plan (1 vCPU, 1.75 GB). Memory averaged 80 % and CPU reached 99 % in 25 working hours that week, and report rendering shares the one core with every page. Change the Web plan size? (issue 982) | **"B2 (2 vCPU, 3.5 GB) (Recommended)"**: `infra/modules/platform.bicep` and ADR-0049 name B2; released by the normal route and measured for a week against the 5 November reading in operations |
| R712 | Same: every Web restart that week was a deployment, 2 to 3 a day in working hours, each one a short stop and a cold start, after which the first e-mail send waited up to 14 s for a token. How should release timing be handled? | **"Keep releasing whenever needed"**: daytime releases stay allowed; the token warm-up (issue 1948) removes the post-release send stall |
| R713 | Test instance (5 November 2026): Robert James instructions arrive as `Re:131809.001/Mr A Ahmadi` with no In-Reply-To or References; the policy read the `Re:` as a reply and left every one Unclassified in No Case Found although the attached instruction proved an inspection. How should Pegasus treat these subjects? | **"Headers decide (Recommended)"**: a `RE:` subject on a message with neither In-Reply-To nor References does not stop a selected instruction that proves its work; with either header, or with no proved work, it stays reply context (FRD-08; `PrincipalMailClassificationPolicy` version 5) |
| R714 | Test instance (5 November 2026): under FRD-08 the classifier agent only classifies and links, so an instruction it recognises (Robert James, a repairer's job) stays in No Case Found until staff click Create case. Should it create the Case itself? | **"Agent creates Cases"**: for an unlinked e-mail it classifies New instruction, the agent runs Create case on the received item (`pegasus_intake_action` `accept`) with the classified work type, the draft's suggested Principal and identity facts the instruction states only where the draft lacks them, accepting Pegasus's own suggested inspection address and never supplying one; a refused Case leaves the e-mail New instruction for staff (FRD-08, FRD-10) |
| R715 | Test instance (5 November 2026): Robert James letters say "Please make arrangements for the inspection with my client. Mr … is available at: Address: …", but the RJS extraction read that block only as the claimant address, so every RJS item waited for staff to type an inspection address (RJS is a physical-address Principal; the `Final` sheet of `providers-worked-on.xlsx` records 66 of its 545 jobs as Image Based Assessment, 10 with no location supplied and the rest at physical locations). Is that address the inspection address? | **"yes it is, teach the rjs extraction this. its the inspection address."**: the address the client "is available at" is the RJS inspection address unless the letter names an inspection or vehicle location itself (FRD-06; `rjs_instruction` version 2) |
| R716 | Dialogs (5 November 2026): every dialog closed when a click landed on its backdrop, and a text selection dragged out of the dialog (the Send report message, for one) and released on the backdrop counted as that click. Should a deliberate click on the backdrop still close a dialog? | **"Keep, fix drag only"**: a click on the backdrop closes the dialog, and the Evidence and Case viewers, only when the press also began on the backdrop; a press inside that ends outside closes nothing |
| R717 | Work Centre System Actions (5 November 2026): the "Matched detail & signal" column (from the v42 Design A mockup) showed "Signal: Automatic association from the recorded case-match decision (principal_case_match v1).", and the "Rules & queries" chip grouped Query opened automatically with Cancellation matched. What should the column, the chips and the stored reason be? | **"dev speak leaking into the UI … I never even said to use signal / matched detail … the whole column is dumb … No idea what 'Rules' is in 'Rules & queries'"**; then **"Delete it entirely (Recommended)"**, **"One chip per kind (Recommended)"** and **"Fix the source text too"**: the table is System action, Case, When and the row actions, and Find matches the reference, registration and vehicle; the chips are All, Auto-matched, Images paired, Query opened automatically and Cancellation matched; a new automatic link's reason no longer names the match policy key and version (the association row still records them), and stored reasons are not rewritten |
| R718 | Intake diagnosis (6 November 2026): the QDOS audit e-mail for Mr Tawqir Ahmed, forwarded inline by desk@ into instructions@, made no Case. Its forwarded header names nduncombe@qdosassist.co.uk; it also carried an older e-mail from PMurphy@qdosassist.co.uk as an attachment, and Pegasus counted both as the original sender and left it for sorting. In a staff inline forward, which sender should count as the original? | **"Inline header wins (Recommended)"**: the sender in the forwarded header is the original; an e-mail it carried as an attachment is just an attachment, like the audit letter and report. FRD-09 "One sender" |
| R719 | Issue #1964 (6 November 2026): the automatic Glass's valuation filed its report as Case document 2 before initial custody recorded the files it had already numbered 2 onwards in Box, so custody failed on the duplicate and the Case stayed Not ready. Three questions: may that unfiled document move to a later number; does the collision keep the shared failure reason; may the stuck test-instance Case `a.PCH26006-p` be retried once the fix runs there | The operator's words, verbatim: **"1,2,3 - agreed on recommendeds"**. (1) Initial custody moves a document on one of its numbers that has no Box file to the Case's next free number; (2) the collision keeps the shared reason "Case evidence could not be stored because the storage service was unavailable."; (3) once the fix runs on the test instance, the Automation Actor retries `a.PCH26006-p`'s Case-source custody |
| R727 | Issue #1852 (6 November 2026): files added from Case AB12CDE with Add evidence filed only after the operator's own edit lease ended (FRD-18 "while the Case is being edited, filing waits for the editor and retries"), and a declared upload whose lease outlasted its five attempts (about 43 minutes) would never file. Four questions: does the lease holder's own upload file at once; does that cover every link the holder makes; does an upload still wait for a colleague's lease; is the 43-minute case fixed in the same issue | The operator's words, verbatim: **"the filing should not wait. If someone uploads, it should be immediately on the case."** and **"1 - yes 2 - yes 3 - no 4 - fix this with the same issue"**. A member of staff's association (Add evidence, Create case, Link to Case) files at once whoever holds the Case's edit lease, and the Case version moves under the lease; the pipeline's own association still waits for a live editor. FRD-18 "Upload for a declared Case", FRD-22 "Staff linking files the material" |
| R728 | Issue #1852, after R727 (6 November 2026): the pipeline's own filing of automatically associated mail still waited for a live editor, and a lease held past its five attempts (about 43 minutes) left the mail's files unfiled; recording an original report recognised in filed material also waits for the editor. Should automatic filing stop waiting too, and does the report recording keep waiting | The operator's words, verbatim: **"1 - yes within this PR"** and **"2 - yes"**. No filing waits for a Case edit lease, the pipeline's included, so the filing rule has no lease input; automatic association and the image merge keep yielding to a live lease (FRD-22), and recording an original report from filed material still waits for the editor and retries. FRD-22 "Staff linking files the material" |
| R729 | Inspection + Audit Cases (6 November 2026): Create audit copied every Inspection decision into the Audit, so its Next action asked to generate the report at once. The operator: **"After opening the audit, need to: If original was Total Loss: 1. Deselect any valuations (but do NOT clear the values). 2. Clear the salvage. If original was repairable: Need to de-select repair spec from original (similar status to if an AI added one via MCP - we keep the original repair spec but it is not set by default)"**, "this will control our 'Next Action' on an Inspection+Audit on the audit case, since currently, when the audit case is created, it prompts to immediately generate a report, but we need to amend some details first". Asked what Deselect keeps: **"dont clear the values from the cards"**; what Clear the salvage covers: **"Salvage value only (Recommended)"**; Cash in lieu, Contract repair or no outcome: **"Treat as Repairable (Recommended)"**; Audits already created: **"New Audits only (Recommended)"** | From the Inspection's outcome at Create audit: a Total loss Audit keeps the guide cards and their values with none Selected, Retail, Trade and Engineer's Value empty and no Salvage value, the category kept; a Repairable, Cash in lieu or Contract repair Audit has every repair spec with none Current; no outcome copies everything. Existing Audits are unchanged, no data migration ([FRD-01](frd/frd-01-case-identity-and-lifecycle.md), [FRD-13](frd/frd-13-case-lifecycle-and-workflow.md#create-audit), [FRD-24](frd/frd-24-engineer-findings-damage-valuation-and-settlement.md), [FRD-25](frd/frd-25-repair-estimates-imports-and-glasss-sessions.md)) |
| R730 | PR #1972 (6 November 2026): the Audit view's Original report card showed the Inspection report's repair cost, salvage value and Engineer's Value, but not its settlement. The operator: **"Under the original report information, we need to show the settlement figure too (it prints on report but its PAV minus salvage)"**, **"for total loss cases only of course"** | The Original report card draws "Settlement" after the Engineer's Value when the Inspection report was a Total loss: the figure that report printed, the pre-accident value less salvage, read from its frozen snapshot ([FRD-16](frd/frd-16-case-record-workspace.md)) |
| R731 | Issue #1890 (4 November 2026): the classifier agent lists a classified `triage-request` with no Case (one whose registration intake could not read, R588) but has no way to act on it. May it open the Triage? | **"Agent opens the Triage when it reads the registration"**: for a Triage request that opened no Triage, the agent may open it with `pegasus_intake_action` `open_triage` and the registration it reads. R588 stays the pipeline's rule (FRD-03, FRD-08) |
| R732 | Same, 6 November 2026: where may the agent read that registration? | **"Subject, body or attachment text (Recommended)"**, and **"Why can it not read photographs claude has image / vision ability..? Choose option 1 + look into the question"**; then, asked how a plate only in photographs is read, **"Pegasus plate reader (Recommended)"**: the registration appears as written in the subject, the body or an attachment's extracted text, or is the one Pegasus's own plate reader agreed on in the e-mail's photographs at the accepted bar; the agent never reads a plate from a photograph itself |
| R733 | Same: R474's photograph read records its reads, reads only a mailed PDF's photographs and runs in the Worker. Where should the photograph read run? | **"Worker reads at intake"**: the Worker also reads the photographs attached beside or instead of a PDF and records the reads as it does today, so mail linking uses them too (R474 widened); the agent's tool only reads what was recorded. Asked whether that covers a pipeline-classified e-mail with no Case that carries only photographs: **"Also photos-only mail"** |
| R734 | Same: the photograph read admits only an e-mail filed as a file with a recognised non-image document, and image automation skips Triage requests, so a Triage request's photographs are never read | **"Worker reads, agent opens (Recommended)"**: the Worker also reads and records the photographs of a Triage request that opened no Triage; the pipeline still leaves it in No Case Found (R588 unchanged); the agent opens the Triage only with the recorded agreed read |
| R735 | Issue #1892 (4 November 2026): from 27 to 30 October the Inbox All filter hid mail from `pegasus_mail_list`, and the agent logged `nothing eligible` every tick. How should a hidden list show? | **"A count on the list, agent warns"**: the list's first page carries a count of Inbox mail the pipeline decided that is Unclassified or has no Case, computed outside the list's filters, and the agent warns when it lists no candidate while the count is above zero |
| R736 | Issue #1916 (6 November 2026): the classifier prompt said old-system folders are named "never by ... a registration", but a PRIVATE one-off's Case reference is its registration and R592 said "primarily" the Pegasus reference. What does the agent search the folders by? | **"Firm ref, incl. PRIVATE reg (Recommended)"**: the firm's own Case reference, which for a PRIVATE one-off is the registration |
| R737 | Same: R583 says "earlier mail in our mailboxes"; the prompt counted only the firm's dealings from before this conversation, not the conversation's own messages or this run's | **"Exclude this conversation (Recommended)"**: earlier mail is the firm's own dealings with the job from before this conversation; the conversation's own messages and this run's batch do not count |
| R738 | Same, with #1915: when both lookups are unavailable the prompt said leave the e-mail; R584 says every Unclassified e-mail the agent reviews ends as correspondence; leaving counted a failed attempt, so a three-tick outage closed mail as failed | **"Leave, retry later (Recommended)"**: the agent leaves it unchanged with no attempt counted and looks at it again next run; an outage never closes mail |
| R739 | Same: the prompt's "never guess, it is correspondence" step read as applying to every eligible e-mail, so it could re-type a pipeline-classified Case-less e-mail on quoted text | **"Unclassified only (Recommended)"**: the step decides Unclassified mail only; an e-mail that already has a type keeps it unless another clearly fits its fresh text |
| R740 | Report names (6 November 2026): the operator's request **"Under contacts -> Report generation We need to be able to customize what the reports get named as for inspections, and audits. Should have options such as {REG} and {case ref} etc along with being able to manually type"**, with the example **"If I set QDOS audits to have a file naming system of: {REG}_{CASE REF}_AUDIT and the case is AB12CDE QDOS26101 filename would be: AB12CDE_QDOS26101_AUDIT.pdf"**. Report sending already had an Attachment name pattern (`{ref}`, `{reg}`, `{outcome}`, First send and Re-send) naming only the e-mailed copy, one for every report. Seven questions were put | Chosen: **"Filed and e-mailed"** (one name on Report generation names the copy filed in Box and Case documents and the attachment; Attachment name leaves Report sending); **"Inspection + Audit, one each"**, then for PCH's seeded `{reg} Initial` / `{reg} Supplementary` **"Keep a re-send template"** (each report has a First send and a Re-send name; a report generated after its work was sent is filed under its Re-send name); `{CASE REF}` prints **"As the report's Our Ref"** (`a.QDOS26101-p`); tokens **"{REG} and {CASE REF}"** and **"{OUTCOME}"**, in any letter case; existing patterns move by **"Additive copy"** into both reports' names; a box shows its default **"Greyed in the empty box"**, after the operator's **"needs to have a default name so that people know what that even is. Also the {REG} and such should be clickable buttons that go into the text box"**; and **"One standard name"** (a Principal without a name files new reports under the e-mail's standard name, `a.QDOS26101-p AB12CDE Repairable report.pdf`; reports already filed keep theirs). Recorded in FRD-04, FRD-11 and FRD-17 |
| R741 | Experian vehicle history check (6 November 2026): which request should Run Experian check send to `POST /v2/vehicle/`, the AutoCheck template (condition, plate changes, keeper and colour changes, stolen, finance, high risk, previous searches) or a Custom Search of chosen blocks? Then: should it also ask for plate changes, which the first request named? | **"we only need condition (write-off) really"**, then **"Condition only"**: a Custom Search with `data.condition` alone; no template, no plate changes, no salvage |
| R742 | Same: what does the check write into the Vehicle history text, which the report prints verbatim? The reference reports' clean sentence names the "Experian AutoCheck database", which a Custom Search is not | **"Sentence + one line each"** and **"Drop 'AutoCheck'"**: clean, "We confirm having checked this vehicle on the Experian database and there is no record of any adverse history."; otherwise "We confirm having checked this vehicle on the Experian database, which records:" then one line per record, "Write-off: {category}, {cause}, loss date {dd/MM/yyyy}, {insurer}, claim {number}", an absent value left out |
| R743 | Same: Experian can return a write-off record an insurer later removed (it carries a date removed). How is a removed record treated? | **"Leave it out"**: only current records are listed; a vehicle whose only records were removed reads as clean |
| R744 | Same: the Vehicle history text may already hold words staff typed. When the check answers, what happens to them? | **"Replace it (Recommended)"**: the check's wording replaces the text; staff may edit it afterwards |
| R745 | Same: should a recorded write-off tick Previous total loss on the Valuation calculator? | **"No, staff tick it (Recommended)"**: the check only reports |
| R746 | Same: where does Run Experian check appear? | **"While editing, with a reg (Recommended)"**: on the Vehicle history sub-panel, in place of "Experian is not connected", only while the Vehicle history text is editable and the Case has a registration; otherwise absent. The not-connected line stays where Experian is not composed |
| R747 | Same: when Experian fails (signed out, refused, down, timed out), the text stays as it was. Which sentence shows? | **"Same pattern (Recommended)"**: "The Experian check is unavailable. Contact an administrator or report a problem." |
| R748 | Same: when Experian answers that it holds no vehicle for the registration (its 404), the text stays unchanged and the page says so as information. Which sentence? | **"Mirror valuation (Recommended)"**: "Experian cannot check this vehicle: it holds no data for its registration." |
| R749 | Same: production releases go out several times a day (R712). How does production treat Experian until its credentials are in Key Vault? | **"Required; PR waits for key (Recommended)"**: the account and endpoints are required Production settings, as Brego's and Cazana's are; the change is built and tested on fakes and waits for the credentials, and the release that ships it carries the Key Vault step |
| R750 | Repair Spec / figure breakdown printout (6 November 2026): the printout took the report's page style (FRD-11, 27 September 2026); the operator supplied an EVA `BREAKDOWN.pdf` | **"We need our generator to EXACTLY match the style and format in the artifacts\breakdown-matching-eva-format example"**: the Repair Spec printout is the EVA breakdown, its logo band, identity lines, line table, EVA band and summary boxes as the example sets them, replacing the report's page style for this document |
| R751 | EVA breakdown (6 November 2026): the example's boxes are Labour/Material/Parts Dis %, Labour/Materials/Parts/Other and their Discount amounts, Total Disc, Nett; Pegasus records Parts, Materials, Specialist and an Overall discount and no labour discount. How do Pegasus's discounts fill them? | **"Before-discount boxes (Recommended)"**: Labour, Materials, Parts and Other print the undiscounted amounts; each discount box prints what that category lost, its own discount plus its share of Overall (Other is Specialist); Total Disc is their sum; Nett unchanged; Labour Disc prints 0.00%, Material Dis and Parts Dis Pegasus's percentages |
| R752 | EVA breakdown (6 November 2026): the example's footer carries EVA's mark, "Document produced using the EVA system", beside the website line. What does Pegasus's footer carry? | **"reproduce the EVA mark (extract it from the PDF - we do have it???)"**: the example's footer band and logo band are the images its PDF carries, printed as it prints them |
| R753 | EVA breakdown (6 November 2026): part numbers, the estimate name, version and status, the source route, Your Ref, Repairer VAT, "To be confirmed", the unpriced Specialist hours, the VAT category wording, the Adjustments section and page numbers have no place in the example. Which survive? | **"Drop them all (Recommended)"**: none of them prints; an unpriced line's cells are blank and the adjustment totals in Other |
| R754 | EVA breakdown (6 November 2026): the example has the logo, identity lines and footer on page 1 and the summary boxes alone on page 2. What does a long table's continuation page carry? | **"Strictly as the example"**: the logo band, the identity lines and the EVA band print on page 1 only; a later page carries the continuing table, its heading repeated, and the summary boxes |
| R755 | Issue #1976 (6 November 2026): three questions PR #1975 (R740) left open. (1) A regenerated report goes into Box as the next version of the series' file, which kept its first name (R664); with report names the name can change between generations, so Case documents and the e-mail showed the new name and Box the old. (2) PCH's seeded attachment name was copied into both its Inspection and Audit names, so an e-mail carrying both of its reports attached two files of one name. (3) The Report generation hint and refusals were used as proposed | Chosen: (1) **"Rename it"**: the new Box version renames the file to the new report's name with its own document number when the report's own name differs from the file's; a document whose name is unchanged keeps the file's first name, as R664 has it; (2) **"Clear PCH's Audit names"**: PCH's Audit report takes the standard name (`a.PCH26118-p AB12CDE Repairable report.pdf`) and its Inspection names stay; the unreleased copy in `20261106180000_PrincipalReportNameColumns` copies PCH's pattern into its Inspection names only; (3) **"Confirm as they are"**: "Use {REG}, {CASE REF} and {OUTCOME}. Empty: the standard name.", "Inspection report name: use only {REG}, {CASE REF} and {OUTCOME}.", "Inspection report name: too long to name a file." (Audit likewise) and the buttons' group label "Placeholders". Recorded in FRD-05 and FRD-11 |
| R756 | Unroadworthy reason (6 November 2026): the operator's request **"In the sentence it is: "Please note the vehicle is unroadworthy as" Change this to: "Please note the vehicle is unroadworthy" We dont want the "as" - this locks the user into a certain way of phrasing. It's just awkward."**; asked how the clause-shaped standard bank wordings fit the shorter opening, and what happens to reasons already saved on Cases | The opening is `Please note the vehicle is unroadworthy` on the Case page and the report, replacing R607's opening; **"Prefix standard wordings with "as""**: the seven standard bank wordings open with "as", and a firm's saved wordings are unchanged; **"Leave them as they are"**: saved reasons are not migrated |
| R757 | Damage markers in Next action (8 November 2026): the operator's request **""Damage markers" under "Next Action" - for this particular alert, we want to offer two options. We want to offer a button for viewing the images using the same route as the popout images function we already have. we want this in the alert"**; asked what the button reads | Chosen option "View images": the Damage markers step draws **View images** beside its Damage control, opening the images window Pop out opens, once one of the Case's images can be read; absent, never disabled, before |
| R758 | Damage marker placement (8 November 2026): **"We want to be able to place the damage markers slightly easier *around* the vehicle, but it seems to cut off right at the edge of its lines, could do with a little more leeway on it"** | A press within 12 plan units of the drawing's outline starts a marker, and a marker's centre may sit up to 12 plan units past the body box (unit-plan margins 12/156 across and 12/394 along). Wider only: no stored marker changes |
| R759 | No mileage (8 November 2026): **"No mileage should also show up early as an issue under next action"**; asked whether it blocks the report or only warns, answered **"prevents review"**; asked how the gate works beside the automatic route, which counts a definitive instruction complete | Chosen option "Mileage-only gate": a Case with no mileage stays Not ready, and is created Not ready, on every route; its own Case requirement, **Vehicle mileage incomplete**, names it and opens the Vehicle section, unless the Instructions row already lists the mileage among its missing items. A lookup or staff edit that records it moves the Case to Review by itself. The automatic route still waives every other instruction item; a Case already in Review or later is not affected, and clearing a mileage never sends a Case back (as R631). The item stays after registration in the asking order |
| R760 | Issue #1845 (8 November 2026): CI is to fail a pull request whose new migration is non-additive unless the body declares it; no pull request has ever named a window, which the release approval names (ADR-0046). What must the body declare? | **"Class and route only (Recommended)"**: a `## Migration` section stating non-additive, each operation and the destructive route; the window stays in the release approval as now |
| R761 | Same, 8 November 2026: a migration's raw SQL (`migrationBuilder.Sql`) cannot be classified by keyword; an `UPDATE` filled a new column in PR 1975 (additive) and rewrote rows in Release 2.0.0 (destructive). How does CI treat a new migration that contains `Sql(...)`? | **"Requires a declaration (Recommended)"**: any `Sql(...)` in `Up` fails unless the body's Migration section states its class; a Drop, Rename or narrowing Alter is non-additive regardless |
| R762 | Issue #1921 (8 November 2026): FRD-10's `pegasus_mail_attachment_text` bullet cites "4 November 2026, #1891" and no ruling; R732 lets the classifier agent read a registration from "Subject, body or attachment text". Which citation does the bullet carry? | **"Cite R732 with #1891 (Recommended)"** |
| R763 | Issue #1919 (8 November 2026): removing a document in Pegasus touches nothing in Box, so a removed document that was the newest of its series stays the series file's current Box version until the next regeneration, which goes on the same file. What should FRD-05 state? | **"State the current behaviour (Recommended)"**: Box keeps the removed document as the file's current version until the next regeneration; earlier versions stay viewable (R664); no code change |
| R764 | Same, 8 November 2026: when Box takes another version during a series upload Pegasus re-reads and retries once; on a second 412 the document stays pending and reconciliation retries it, but the staff caller sees the red "could not be stored in Box just now" error. What should staff see? | **"Answer Pending instead"**: a second 412 answers the amber "still being filed to Box" outcome rather than an error |
| R765 | Same, 8 November 2026: if version B reached Box but its confirmation was lost and C is then filed on the same series, reconciliation files B as a file of its own once C is confirmed, but puts B on top of C while C is still pending. How is it handled? | **"Fix it in this PR"**: reconciliation of an older version never overtakes a newer pending one of its series; it is filed as a file of its own |
| R766 | Issue #1950 (8 November 2026): the Send to AI switch is read on every Case open; the same port is how `CreateAiJob` refuses new jobs when the switch is off, and a Worker copy could only expire by lifetime. Cache it, or leave it uncached? | **"Cache it, 30 s lifetime"**: the workflow configuration cache's pattern (R342); the Administration save evicts Web's copy so the cut is immediate there, and any other copy expires within 30 s |
| R767 | Issue #1997 (9 November 2026): no FRD, ruling or ADR traced the 200-line bound on a Repair Spec, which every path that records a spec (Case save, MCP and AI-draft saves, every import) already enforced; should it stay, rise or go? And since the Case page form and the printout must still carry a spec, what is the ceiling? | **"Drop the business bound"**; the ceiling is **"500 lines (Recommended)"**: one Core number, a technical limit for the Case form and the printout and not a business rule, on every path that records a spec |
| R768 | Issue #1970 (9 November 2026): Release 2.2.0's Query Store after window. Releases 2.3.0 and 2.3.1 followed, and only Friday afternoon ran on exactly 2.2.0's bytes. Which 24 h window is read? | **"Fri 12:43Z → Sat 12:43Z"**: the after window runs from 12:43Z on 6 November to 12:43Z on 7 November, on 2.2.0's bytes only, mostly a weekend |
| R769 | Issue #1961 (9 November 2026): the page p95 reading after a week on B2 is due on 13 November, and the slowness PRs #1989 to #1992, whose after figures were owed there too, have not shipped. How is #1961 handled? | **"Partial reading now"**: the about-three-day reading from 6 November settles #1961; the after figures for #1989 to #1992 move to #2000 |
| R770 | Issue #1951 (8 November 2026): a Case save ends the edit lease inside its transaction and the page claims the next one in a second transaction (about six statements and a second lock per save). Include a lease carry-over in #1951? FRD-14 (R514) says every staff change ends the token it was made under, so a still-matching token proves nothing landed, and a save in one of the holder's two windows must make the other window's heartbeat refuse; keeping the same token would break both. Which writes keep the lease this way? | **"Include it in #1951"**; **"Yes, next token in the save"**: the lease is kept and renewed (holder, expiry and any standing take-over request) and its next token is issued inside the save's own transaction, with no claim and no second lock afterwards; **"The Case field save only"**: Actions-menu items, estimate commands and document actions taken inside a session keep today's end-then-claim-again. Names R550: its "the holder's own save, which ends and re-claims the lease" no longer applies to a Case save. FRD-14, FRD-16 and ADR-0064 amended |
| R771 | Issue #1952 (8 November 2026): when the fee note fails after the report succeeded, the Case page shows the fee note's failure beside the report's success and the MCP tool fails the whole call, although its own comment says the report stands whatever the fee note's outcome. Should MCP change? | **"Leave both as they are"**: #1952 adds the shared custody batch and the one pairing condition only; each caller keeps its own fee-note failure handling |
| R772 | Issue #1949 (8 November 2026): held on 6 November for #1961's B2 reading; its investigation rejected the fix as written (one Overview image for page 1 and the image page would change page 1's pinned resolution) and left two options. Keep the hold, prepare photos in parallel, or keep prepared photos between renders? And the FRD-11 sentence R305 asked to have drafted for approval | **"Build parallel photo prepare"**: `PrepareAsync` prepares at most two photos at a time (`QuestPdfAssessmentReportRenderer.ImagesPreparedAtOnce`), in the photos' order, under the render's token; the Overview is still opened once and prepared for both its slot and the image pages. FRD-11's sentence **approved as drafted**: "The renderer opens a source image only when it prints that image, prepares at most two at a time, and lets each go once it is prepared, so a long report never holds every source at once." |
| R773 | Guided photo capture (9 November 2026): the PRD excludes SMS (BND-04) and automatic chasers (MAIL-19); the handover's app sends its link by text, WhatsApp or e-mail with automatic reminders. How are links and reminders sent? | Chosen: **"E-mail + copy link, staff-clicked reminders"**: HQ e-mails the link from an approved mailbox through the Graph staff send with a new Photo link template; text and WhatsApp are Copy link and the staff member's own phone; Links we sent shows what is due; Resend is one click; no provider and no automatic send ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md), FRD-21) |
| R774 | Same: where do photo capture, its photos and a contact's standing link live in the application? | Chosen: **"Rail item without count; Files → Images; Contacts tab"**: a Photo capture rail item under Work with no badge; captured photos in the Case's Files → Images in story order with the customer's note; standing links and their defaults on a Photo links tab of the contact's edit page (FRD-12, FRD-16, FRD-04, FRD-17) |
| R775 | Same: how are captured photos filed in the Case's Box folder? | Chosen: **"Flat folder, story-order names, baskets = In report"**: no subfolders; names such as `014 02-03 Front driver corner closer.jpg`; inspection images arrive In report, additional information (scene, other vehicle, paperwork, video) not In report, the other vehicle tagged Third party; moving between the baskets is the In report toggle (FRD-05) |
| R776 | Same: how does a capture that arrives before its instruction find its Case? | Chosen: **"Reuse FRD-19 and FRD-22 as they stand"**: one eligible Case whose incident date does not contradict, attach; none, a Vehicle images record (Awaiting instruction, its own Box folder, pairs on the instruction, the existing chase interval); several, staff choose on the existing Vehicle images record page; no new check-the-match state (FRD-19, FRD-22) |
| R777 | Same: how is the feature delivered? | **"One branch, one PR"** |
| R778 | Same: the Razor mockup gate before Stage 2 | **"Prototype stands as the mockup; sign off on PR screenshots"**: the handover's app is the mockup, and the staff pages are signed off on the pull request's screenshots |
| R779 | Same: which hostname do links carry, and how is the hosted test instance reached from a phone? | Chosen: **"Pegasus hostname; Funnel the capture paths on the test instance"**: links are `https://pegasus-prod-web-252ow37gij.azurewebsites.net/c/{token}` and `/s/{slug}`; R676 is amended so the hosted test instance's Tailscale Funnel on 8443 also exposes `/c`, `/s` and `/capture` while `Features__PhotoCapture` is on there; a custom domain stays a separate decision (EXT-19 stays retired) (ADR-0090, ADR-0088) |
| R780 | Same: what is in the first release? | Chosen: **"Website standing link for private customers, Video and paperwork from the app, Full-size originals in the background, QR code for standing links"**: all four |
| R781 | Same: when do a photo link's photos complete the Case's images? | Chosen: **"When the capture settles"**: when the sender presses Send, or the link expires with photos, and every photo has filed; never on the first photo (FRD-13) |
| R782 | Same: what reference does the phone app show the customer? | Chosen: **"Registration only, no reference"**: the app shows the registration and never a Case/PO or any other reference (BND-06) |
| R783 | Same: what happens to the details the sender types? | Chosen: **"Fill empty Case fields with 'Photo link' provenance"**: claimant name, contact number, accident date, inspection location and mileage fill empty Case fields as Facts with source kind Photo link; a value already there is never overwritten; the views with no Case field (roadworthy, preferred outcome, in hire, airbags, steering) stay on the capture and show with the photos (FRD-23) |
| R784 | Same: issue #1683 (guided capture and upload links), parked by R464 and closed on 6 November 2026, is built. Which capability identities does it take? | New ones: EXT-21 (the capture app and public surface), INT-34 (the capture intake channel, routing and filing), MAIL-27 (the photo link e-mail and staff-clicked reminders) and UI-20 (the Photo capture workspace); the retired EXT-16, INT-31 and DOC-06 are never reused ([capabilities](capabilities.md)) |
| R785 | Same, amending R676: which paths does the hosted test instance's Funnel on 8443 expose? | Chosen, under R779: **"Pegasus hostname; Funnel the capture paths on the test instance"**: `/hooks/microsoft-graph/mail` and, while `Features__PhotoCapture` is on there, `/c`, `/s` and `/capture`; port 443 stays tailnet-only, and `PhotoCapture__PublicOrigin` is the `:8443` Funnel origin |
| R786 | Issue #2008 (9 November 2026): the capture app shows one sentence the handover prototype never needed, when the phone cannot reach Pegasus or a refusal carries no words of its own. Which words? | Chosen: **"Keep it"**: "We couldn’t reach Collision Engineers. Check your signal and try again." |
| R787 | Same: after Send, with items still missing, the app opens a sheet headed "Could you add a few more?" listing them, with the buttons "Add more" and "That’s everything" (handover item 6 left the words unconfirmed). Keep them? | Chosen: **"Keep them"**: the heading "Could you add a few more?" and the buttons "Add more" and "That’s everything" |
| R788 | Same: the photo link e-mail's subject, built as the registration alone ("AB12 CDE", or "Photo link" with none). What should it be? | Chosen: **"Photos of {registration} for Collision Engineers"**: for example "Photos of AB12 CDE for Collision Engineers"; with no registration, "Photos for Collision Engineers" |
| R789 | Same: the page an unknown, expired, cancelled or paused link shows is headed "This link is no longer active." and, as built, adds "Ask whoever sent it to you for a new one." What beyond the heading? | Chosen: **"Keep the second line"**: "Ask whoever sent it to you for a new one.", the same for all four causes |
