| R790 | Ship-readiness review of guided photo capture, PR #2005 (9 November 2026): a link sent from a Case filed its photos as the routing's system work, so on a report-sent, closed or Triage Case they were linked and never filed. Who decides where a Case link's photos go? | Chosen: **"Case links are a staff decision: always file"**: "The member of staff who sent the link is the decider, so the photos file on any Case that is not archived, including report-sent, closed and Triage Cases. Only a bodyshop/AMC capture that Pegasus routed is system work." Amends FRD-28's "recorded as the routing's system work (R776)" for a Case link ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md)) |
| R791 | Same: a standing link's Principal and the typed accident date were applied at the start and dropped at the settle and at later pairing. How does a capture with no Case find one after its start? | Chosen: **"Re-match at settle with scope and date"**: "At settle Pegasus matches again with the link's Principal and the typed date: one eligible Case files straight on it (no Vehicle images record); none or several makes the record, which carries the typed date so a later instruction with a contradicting date never pairs. No Principal check at later pairing (FRD-19 as it stands) and no extra scope for Claim Source or Repairer links." (FRD-28, FRD-19, FRD-22) |
| R792 | Same: when does a batch settle, and what may repeat on a second Send? | Chosen: **"Settle: filed, once per capture, no empty settles"**: "A batch settles when every photo in it has filed in Box. Images complete once per capture, only for a batch with photos, and never after staff marked them incomplete. The note is written once; a changed note is appended. The bell rings for each batch that brought photos. A Send with no photos settles nothing." (FRD-28, FRD-13) |
| R793 | Same: what happens to a photo link whose Case is archived while the link is live? | Chosen: **"Archived Case: the link goes dead"**: "Once the Case is archived the link answers 'This link is no longer active' (the same page as expired or cancelled) and takes nothing more. Photos already filed stay on the Case. Links we sent shows the capture as Expired." |
| R794 | Same: when staff cancel a link that already has photos, do those photos still settle? | Chosen: **"Cancel stops uploads only"**: "The link dies, and the photos already received still settle as normal: a Case capture completes images, writes the note and rings the bell; a bodyshop capture becomes its Vehicle images record." |
| R795 | Same: where does Cancel live? | Chosen: **"Cancel on list rows and the Case page too"**: "A Cancel action on each live row of Links we sent and beside the Case page's photo link mention, as well as on the link page. Same confirmation, same refusal sentence." |
| R796 | Same: can a standing link be ended outright, and does an inactive contact's link keep working? | Chosen: **"Retire action; a deactivated contact's link stops"**: "Retire ends a standing link for good (its address answers 'no longer active' and cannot be resumed), and a link whose contact is deactivated answers the same until the contact is active again." |
| R797 | Same: where is the website link managed, does it get a defaults form, and what is its readable address? R780 names the website link as a first-release feature and no placement. | Chosen: **"Website link: keep the panel; add defaults and a typed address"**: "Administrators keep managing it on the Photo capture page, gain a defaults form (live camera only, lifetime, originals) and type the readable address themselves in the panel (checked for shape and uniqueness); the code never invents one. The R780 citation becomes this ruling." |
| R798 | Same: a quick-mode link sent from a Case opened the private customer's start page, with no staff form and no "Send customer a link". Build the handover's staff version? | Chosen: **"Build the staff version of a quick Case link"**: "A quick-mode Case link opens the staff start and form from the handover app, and 'Send customer a link' mints a guided link on the same Case, carried by the staff member's own phone; Links we sent shows it as sent on to the customer." |
| R799 | Same: the app lets a sender with no keys skip the dashboard photo, and Pegasus then listed it as missing. Is the dashboard required then? | Chosen: **"Dashboard not required when they say no keys"**: "The skip is recorded on the capture, the dashboard is not listed as missing, and staff see 'Skipped – no keys available', the handover app's own words." |
| R800 | Same: the app had one extras row for the other vehicle and the scene together, so R775's Third party tag was unreachable. A row of its own? | Chosen: **"Separate 'The other vehicle' row"**: "The extras list gains a row for the other vehicle using the handover app's own words, and 'Accident scene photos' keeps 'where it happened'; other-vehicle photos arrive tagged Third party and out of the report." |
| R801 | Same, narrowing R780's "Full-size originals in the background": an original for a capture with no Case could never file and the phone retried until the link died. What happens to originals there? | Chosen: **"Originals: compressed only until there is a Case"**: "The app is told originals are off while the capture has no Case, so it sends and retries nothing; when the record later reaches a Case the compressed pictures stand, as FRD-28 already allows when an original fails." |
| R802 | Same: damage photos taken outside a marked area ("Add more", the phone's own camera) carried no area or stage and were refused and lost. Where do they go? | Chosen: **"Loose damage photos go to a 'Damage' group after the marked areas"**: "Pegasus accepts them; they file in a Damage group after the areas (Box name like '015 04-03 Damage.jpg'), count towards no area's required stages, and the app keeps the handover's 'Other photos' heading. No new words." |
| R803 | Same: paperwork and video sent on a capture with no Case. Refuse them, or keep them? | Chosen, over refusing them: **"Paperwork and video on a capture with no Case are kept for the Case"**: "Paperwork and video are held with the waiting photos and filed as Other documents when the record reaches a Case. A new storage and linking path; the record page would not show them." |
| R804 | Same: FRD-28 rings the Engineer's bell when a photo link's Vehicle images record reaches a Case; FRD-19's pairing rings none. Which stands? | Chosen: **"The bell rings when a photo-link record reaches a Case"**: "FRD-28 stands: when a photo-link record pairs with a Case the Engineer's bell rings once, and FRD-19 gains 'except a photo link's capture (FRD-28)'." |
| R805 | Same: wordings the build shows that no ruling had listed. Keep them? | Chosen: **"Wording: keep them all as listed"**: the reopen line "You've already sent us {N} photos – add more?" (singular fixed); the nudge buttons "Add more photos" and "Continue with {n}"; the after-Send list items "No overview photos", "Overview {n} of 8", "{area}: overview/closer/close-up", "No number plate photo", "No dashboard photo" and "No VIN photo"; the server refusal sentences the phone shows, as they stand in `PhotoCaptureUseCases.cs`, `CaptureEndpoints.cs`, `CaptureLinkAdmission.cs` and `DocumentUploads.cs`; the other vehicle row "Other vehicle photos" / "The other vehicle involved" with the scene sub-line "Where it happened"; Links we sent From is the Case's Principal, the Case column reads "Awaiting instruction" for a capture with no Case, and the history lines are "Cancelled · name", "Link extended · name" and "Call logged · name"; Retire is the button "Retire link" with the confirmation "Retire this link? Its address stops working for good and cannot be resumed."; the Intake log reasons are "Destination declared by the photo link sent from this Case.", "Destination chosen from the registration typed on the photo link." and "Photo link item: waiting for its capture to be sent; no identification ran." |
| R806 | Review of the PR #2005 plan against the handover's HQ prototype (9 November 2026): the staff pages differ from the prototype in about twenty places no ruling covers. Which groups are built into the PR before the first release? | Chosen, three of four: **"Editable saved addresses"** ("On a contact's Photo links tab, add, edit and remove saved inspection addresses on the spot, as the prototype does", knowing "this makes a second place where addresses are kept"); **"List extras"** ("'Ask for it' on Coming in rows with something missing (today only Links we sent can Remind), counts on the two tabs, and a 'No case yet' filter"); **"Send and Case extras"** ("Send: a preview of the message with the customer's name filled in, and 'Put the standard wording back'. Case page: the link's status beside the photos, and 'No photos yet' with 'Resend the link' when nothing has arrived"). Not chosen: "Hints and subtitles", recorded as an issue and not built |
| R807 | Same, completing the kept paperwork and video ruling above: what happens to kept files when staff close the record or it never pairs, or when a sender sent only paperwork so nothing settles? | Chosen: **"Open from the link's page"**: "They stay kept and unfiled, and the link's page lets staff open each one, so nothing received is out of reach. Nothing new is filed, chased or deleted; a paperwork-only capture stays on Coming in as it is today." |
| R808 | Same: the handover has two start pages for a quick link sent from a Case, the instructing firm's and the bodyshop's, and the Send page records neither. Which does a quick Case link open, and what is the choice labelled? | Chosen: **"Ask on the Send page"**: "When Quick is chosen, Send asks who the link is for, 'Accident management' or 'Bodyshop' (the prototype's own words), and each gets its handover page and form. One more choice on Send; the VIN stays required only for a bodyshop's staff." The choice is labelled **"Type"**, the prototype's own label for it on a link profile |
| R809 | Same: after Send the app says "Forgot something? Open the same link again to send more photos for this vehicle.", which on a standing link starts a new capture. What should a capture started from a standing link say? | Chosen: **"Different sentence there"**, in the words **"Forgot something? Open the link again and enter the same registration to send more photos for this vehicle."**; a link sent from a Case or sent on to a customer keeps the handover's sentence |
| R810 | Same, closing the open decision on a typed mileage (FRD-23): where does a mileage typed on a photo link rank, and what does the report say of it? | Chosen: **"Above estimate, principal sentence"**: "It ranks below anything from the instruction and above the DVSA estimate, so the estimate never replaces it", and "the report keeps 'advised by our instructing principal' whoever typed it. Nothing to build beyond the rank." |
| R811 | Same, closing the open decision on a typed inspection location (FRD-06) and narrowing R783: does a location typed on a photo link fill the Case's inspection address? | Chosen: **"Do not fill the location"**: "It stays on the capture with the photos, and staff type the address if they want it. This narrows R783 for the inspection location." |
| R812 | Same, held from the PR's second comment: should finished links leave the Photo capture lists after a time? | Chosen: **"Keep them all for now"**: "No change for the release. The measurement issue reports the lists' speed at today's count and at 2,000 captures, and a cut-off is decided on that number." |
| R813 | Same, held from the PR's second comment and amending FRD-28's "The limits are constants, not configuration": should any of the reminder timings, the lifetimes, the daily caps or the Send page's starting values become a setting in Administration › Configuration? | Chosen, two of four: **"Send's starting values"** ("Whether Send starts on Guided or Quick, and with Live camera only and originals on or off") and **"Lifetimes and the default"** ("Which lifetimes Send offers and which one starts selected"). The reminder timings and the daily caps stay constants |
| R814 | Same, held from the PR's second comment: should Remind have wording of its own? | Chosen: **"Keep one template"**: "Remind sends the same e-mail with a fresh link. Nothing to build and one template to maintain." |
| R815 | Same: once saved addresses can be added and edited on a contact's Photo links tab, what happens to the entries shown there automatically today (the contact's own address, or a Principal's inspection locations)? | Chosen: **"Keep them beside the new ones"**: "The automatic entries stay read-only and still appear on the firm's form. Addresses added on the tab are listed with them and are the only ones that can be edited or removed. Nothing shown today disappears." |
| R816 | Same: what does "Ask for it" on a Coming in row do, given R773 and that a capture with no Case has no Case mailbox? | Chosen: **"E-mail if possible, else copy"**: "It makes a fresh link for that capture. When the capture is on a Case and holds an e-mail address it e-mails the link, as Resend does; otherwise it shows the link for staff to copy and send themselves." |
| R817 | Same: what may an Administrator change in the lifetimes setting? | Chosen: **"Which of 3, 7, 14, and the default"**: "Tick which of the three Send offers and pick the one that starts selected. No new numbers; Extend and a contact's link defaults keep all three." |
| R818 | Issue #2003 (9 November 2026): the Worker's thumbnail candidate read went from about 6 ms to 34 ms a run, once a minute, because SQL scans `DocumentContentCacheEntries` once per image version filed in the last 30 days; it serves no page, and the efficiency rule builds a change only when it brings a page under budget or saves 100 ms at p95 on one. Record and wait, or build an index anyway? | **"Build an additive index now"**: the unique `(DocumentVersionId, Variant)` index on `DocumentContentCacheEntries` includes `ExpiresAtUtc`, an exception to the page rule for this Worker statement. (The 100 ms-per-run re-read trigger, chosen for the record-and-wait path, was not needed.) |
| R819 | U74 on the test instance (9 November 2026): a body shop's chaser ("M500ULD") whose only images were inline signature banners at 400x200 and 540x302 passed the photograph size rule, so the e-mail was image material and became Unidentified; FRD-19 accepted "a large one-off signature graphic, which staff then sort by hand". The instance's mail showed the same for the QDOS Assistance logo (200x200, four e-mails) and a Sutton Car Repairs banner (3319x1181). How should Pegasus stop this? | **"Both"**: an image inline in an e-mail body is a photograph only at a shortest side of at least 400 px and sides under 2.5:1 (attached and embedded images keep 200 px and 3:1), and the four graphics join the known graphics |
| R820 | Same: signature graphics already filed as Case images (the QDOS Assistance logo twice on the test instance; production probably more). What happens to them? | **"Leave as is"**: the change applies to new mail only; staff remove any they notice by hand, and nothing is recorded |
| R821 | Issue #1914 (9 November 2026): on the Audit view of an Inspection + Audit Case, FRD-16 and the live code head the Send report dialog "Report · a.{Case/PO}", the Audit report's own name, while the v48 mockup's Audit dialog draws "Report · {Case/PO}" without "a.". Which does the eyebrow show? | **"Report · a.{Case/PO} (Recommended)"**: the eyebrow names the report the dialog sends; FRD-16 and the code are unchanged |
| R822 | Issue #1834 (9 November 2026), question 1: the Oakwood profile already exists and the live letter template creates Cases at the tip; four real Oakwood letters of 9 November 2026 (M. Norris, A. Fox, G. Henry, J. Porteous, both accepted domains) each selected `oak_instruction`, classified `new-instruction` / Inspection and created OAK26001-p to OAK26004-p on the hosted test instance at the dev-inclusive head. The 30 October letter is pre-cutover and not reproducible. Are the four live letters the proof and the holdouts? | Chosen: **"Yes, the four 9 Nov letters"**: one development letter and three holdouts; no pull of the 27 and 30 October e-mails and no re-run through the test instance |
| R823 | Same, question 2: the tell wording the done-when asks to confirm. The code's tells are the document signature (`Oakwood`, `Client reg:` and `Client model:` in one letter) and the inspection sentence "Please arrange an inspection of my client's vehicle as soon as possible", which FRD-09's explicit-inspection rule matches | Chosen: **"Yes, record those tells as stated"**: these are the Oakwood tells; FRD-09's Classification clause already covers them, so no FRD change |
| R824 | Same, question 3: the deliverable, given that OAK has no CI fact proving classification or the live RTF letter layout end to end | Chosen: **"Small PR: CI test + ruling row"**: one fact in `OakInstructionExtractionPolicyTests` pinning the live layout to OAK selection, extraction and NewInstruction / Inspection, plus these rows; no profile or predicate change; `Closes #1834` |
| R825 | Issue #1836 (9 November 2026): the DFD route, instruction profile and classification tell have existed since 8 September 2026 and the hosted test instance made Cases DFD26001-p, DFD26002-p and DFD26003-p from three real DFD e-mails on 6 and 9 November, but no ruling records the tell and FRD-09 names only "the DFD/FW instruction template"; which wording is recorded, and is Inspection the only work the form instructs? | Chosen: **"Form title"** and **"Inspection only"**: an e-mail from `dfd-solicitors.co.uk` whose attached form is titled `Engineer instruction request` is a new instruction for an Inspection; the subject line plays no part. Recorded in FRD-09 and delivered as a docs and test PR (**"Docs + test PR"**) |
| R826 | Same, 9 November 2026: the three live receipts show two DFD extraction defects outside the issue's text. The incident date is never read: the policy mapped it to form field `Text7`, while the live form carries the accident date in `Text16` and the date instructed in `Text17`, both of which the policy gave to Additional information. The claimant contact is read as the printed line "(Below is optional)" when the form's contact field is blank. Raise a follow-up issue, or fix here? | Chosen: **"Fix in this PR"**: `dfd_instruction` v2 reads the incident date from `Text16` (keeping `Text7` for the earlier template), Additional information from `Text15` alone, claims `Text17` as the date instructed, and treats "(Below is optional)" as a boundary, not a value |
| R827 | Release 2.4.1 (9 November 2026): the test instance serves #2005's head, whose PhotoCapture migration has run on its database, so it cannot return to `dev`'s tip without #2005 landing or a Reset, and the preflight refuses a candidate the instance is not serving (R684). Reset the instance, hold the release for #2005, or stop? | **"just release dev anyway? we dont need to do the test instance?"**: Release 2.4.1 was built and released without the instance serving the candidate; the R684 check was skipped in a local copy of the release script for this release only, and R684 stands |
| R828 | Same: the preflight proposed MINOR 2.5.0 (R687: #2004 closes #1949, a `type:task`); the operator asked for 2.4.1 | **"Approve as 2.4.1"**: the release is 2.4.1, tagged `v2.4.1` |
| R829 | Issue #1837 (9 November 2026): the real Baker & Coleman letter of 6 November (`Engineer Ltr RTA Initial Instructions.DOC`, test instance receipt `f017a845`) was selected by the BC profile but stayed Unclassified under `principal_mail_classification` v5, because its request reads "Please would you prepare a desktop report of your findings." with no inspect or examine word, and the classifier agent made Case BC26001-p by hand; the April 2026 letters say "arrange to inspect our client's vehicle" and classify. Which wording is Baker & Coleman's accepted instruction tell? | Chosen: **"'prepare a desktop report'"**: BC-only, case-insensitive, inside the selected letter, as the DFD and FW template tells are; classifies new-instruction / Inspection (`principal_mail_classification` v6) |
| R830 | Same, 9 November 2026: only one real desktop-wording letter exists locally (the test instance's 6 November one); the reference pack's five letters are the physical wording. How is the holdout obtained? | Chosen: **"Pull BC mail from engineers@ now"** through `pegasus-corpus-holding`; the held letters run through the real reader and policy as the holdout |
| R831 | Same, 9 November 2026: the BC extraction surfaces "Requested inspection method: Physical vehicle inspection" only for the physical wording; a desktop letter surfaces nothing, and Baker & Coleman's notes say physical instructions need Service Certainty. Surface a desktop value in this PR? | Chosen: **"No, classification only"**; a follow-up issue if wanted |
| R832 | Issue #1835 (9 November 2026): every real Blackstone Legal instruction held from `engineers@` (three, 25 September to 15 October 2026, from both `info@` "Our ref: N / New instructions" and `Claims@` "Our Client - name / Our Reference - N") carries the letter sentence "Please accept this correspondence as formal instructions to inspect our clients vehicle and prepare a report detailing the costs of repair or total loss value, if beyond economical repair."; the e-mail body only says "Please see attached new instructions." and Pegasus already classifies the letter through the explicit-inspection predicate. What is Blackstone's tell for the record? | **"The letter's 'formal instructions to inspect' sentence"**: FRD-09 records that letter sentence as Blackstone's tell, matched by the existing `instruction.explicit-inspection` predicate; no classification change |
| R833 | Issue #1835 (9 November 2026): the real letters differ from the reference-pack letters in two ways `black_instruction` v1 missed. All three put "Our ref: N" after the signature, where the profile did not read, so every Case was allocated with no claim number; one of three wrote the vehicle as "Make Model REG" with no dash, so that Case had no registration or make. The Case was still allocated from the claimant's name and accident date. Fix these in the same PR? | **"Fix both in this PR"**: `black_instruction` v2 reads the reference after the signature as well as above the salutation, and accepts an undashed terminal registration when it is a dated shape; the live letter's text is the test fixture |
| R834 | Issue #1835 (9 November 2026): how is the evidence gathered and proved? The route, Principal and profile already existed but no Pegasus decision for a Blackstone e-mail existed anywhere (production's staff mailboxes are outgoing-only; the test instance began retaining `engineers@` after the last Blackstone e-mail). | **"Corpus-holding pull"** of Blackstone mail from `engineers@` with the operator's Graph sign-in; **"Both shapes"** (`info@` and `Claims@` subjects) in scope; **"Core evaluation only"** over every held e-mail with the counts in the PR, no seeding of the test instance; **"Pull all folders first"** when the inbox alone held three; the all-folders pull found no more, because the other Blackstone originals had been deleted from `engineers@` and only the staff replies quoting them remain, so the three are the whole available population |
| R835 | Follow-ups to guided photo capture (10 November 2026; issues #2020 to #2026 and #2029, and the operator's own test on Case YML26003-p): the nine issues hold about 45 decisions that are the operator's. How are they taken, and how is the work split? | Chosen: **"Rounds of four, now"** and **"Three pull requests"**: "One for the photo capture pages and app (#2022, #2023, #2024, #2026, #2029, and the delay you saw on YML26003-p), one for image intake (#2021), one for the list speed (#2020). #2025 and #2009 need no code." |
| R836 | Same, #2025: the test instance holds photo capture rows written by the build it ran before the ship-readiness fixes (eighteen item rows with no receipt, two captures settled the old way, filing plans without the hold-back flag, one website link with a random address). What happens to them? | Chosen: **"Leave them"**: "Nothing is changed on the instance. #2025 records that the rows stay and what the two effects are." The two effects: a capture settled then is never picked up if its record later reaches a Case, and the website link keeps its random address until an Administrator replaces it |
| R837 | Same, #2022: the app holds photos not yet sent only in the page's memory, so a reload or a discarded tab loses them. Should photos not yet sent survive a reload? | Chosen: **"Leave as it is"**: "Photos already sent are safe; the sender retakes any that were still waiting. No change to the app, and FRD-28 states the behaviour." |
| R838 | Same, the operator's test on YML26003-p: Send was pressed at 08:48:47 and the Case moved to Review at 08:50:00, because the settle ran only every fifth minute. How soon should a Case move after Send? | Chosen: **"Within a minute"**: "The settle runs on the Worker's every-minute timer. When nothing is waiting it costs three or four small database reads a minute. The Case moves within about a minute of Send, once every photo has filed." (FRD-28, `docs/current-architecture.md`) |
| R839 | Same, the operator on YML26003-p: "Box also seems to store the images twice (can see two versions)". The two versions are one Box file: the small picture, then the full-size original as its next version (R780, R801). Should this stay? | Chosen: **"Keep it"**: "The engineer sees each photo within seconds and the full-size one replaces it. Box keeps the small picture in that file's version history. Nothing to build." |
| R840 | Same, #2026 items 1 and 29: R792 says a batch settles when every photo in it has filed, and some photos can never file. Does such a photo hold its batch? | Chosen: **"Never-filing photo does not hold"**: "As built, confirmed as your rule: the batch settles on the photos that filed. The rare staff-linked photo that never starts filing keeps its batch waiting." This replaces the lead's reading recorded in FRD-28 and FRD-13 on 9 November |
| R841 | Same, item 2: R792 says a capture completes its Case's images once and never after staff marked them incomplete, but a later Vehicle images record of the same capture completed them again when it merged. Which holds? | Chosen: **"Once per capture"**: "R792 holds: a capture that has already completed a Case's images does not complete them again when its later record merges. FRD-28's sentence is corrected." |
| R842 | Same, item 4: should Ask for it (R816) count as a reminder? | Chosen: **"Counts as a reminder"**: "As built. The reminder count and the history treat it like Remind; nothing changes." |
| R843 | Same, items 10 and 42: R783 says a typed detail fills only an empty Case field; R810 ranks a typed mileage above the DVSA estimate, which is usually written first (OAK26003-p kept 145,137 Estimated against a typed 48,250). Which rule wins for mileage? | Chosen: **"Typed mileage replaces an estimate"**: "R810 wins: a mileage typed on a photo link replaces a DVSA estimate, and only that. A value from the instruction or one staff entered is never replaced." (FRD-23, FRD-28) |
| R844 | Same, items 12 and 26: kept paperwork and video that follow a record Pegasus itself paired are system work, which never files into a Triage Case or a report-sent Case, so they stayed linked and unfiled. What happens to them? | Chosen: **"File it where the photos went"**: "R803 holds: kept paperwork and video file on whatever Case the record reached, a Triage Case or a report-sent Case included, as R790 already does for links sent from a Case." |
| R845 | Same, item 9: a link sent from a Triage Case wrote the customer's note nowhere, because a Triage Case has no Case Notes. Where does the note go, given a Triage note holds 500 characters and a finished Triage Case takes none? | Chosen: **"Write it as a Triage note"**: "The note is added to the Triage Case's own notes with the same words a Case note gets ("Customer's note (photo link): …")." For a longer note the operator first chose "Widen Triage notes to 2,000" and withdrew it on being told that an AlterColumn is non-additive (R760, R761); final choice **"Several notes instead"**: "A longer note is written as up to four Triage notes of 500 characters each, in order. Nothing is lost, there is no database change, and the release stays on the normal route." Nothing is written to a completed or cancelled Triage Case; the whole note stays on the photo link's page (FRD-28, FRD-03) |
| R846 | Same, item 14: FRD-04 says every change to a contact is a permanent action-history event, and changes to a standing photo link recorded nothing. Which is right, and what are the events called? | Chosen: **"Record them"**: "FRD-04 holds: each of those acts writes a history event as other Contacts changes do, the website link's included." Names, chosen **"Use these"**: "Photo link created", "Photo link paused", "Photo link resumed", "Photo link replaced", "Photo link retired", "Photo link defaults saved", "Photo link address added", "Photo link address changed", "Photo link address removed"; the website link's events sit under the area "Website photo link" and a contact's under Contact. "Creating a link is recorded too, since FRD-04 says every change." |
| R847 | Same, item 15: the ribbon hid Send photo link on a closed Case while the Send page still opened by its address. Should a closed Case be able to send a photo link? | Chosen: **"No: hide and refuse"**: "The button stays hidden and the Send page refuses a closed Case with the existing sentence "The Case cannot take a photo link." FRD-16 and FRD-28 say so. Links sent before it closed keep working." |
| R848 | Same, item 6: after a hand-on, should Pegasus record and show that the customer has not opened the link yet? | Chosen: **"Leave it"**: "As built: the row shows 'Sent on to their customer' and the firm's Opened until photos arrive. Nothing to build." |
| R849 | Same, item 8: a Bodyshop quick link sent from a Case names no firm, so three lines an Accident management link shows are left out. Should it name the firm? | Chosen: **"Leave them out"**: "As built: with no firm named, those three lines are not shown on a Bodyshop link." |
| R850 | Same, item 28: when photos arrive on a Case with no Engineer the bell is dropped. Should an Engineer assigned later be told? | Chosen: **"Leave it"**: "As built: no bell when there is nobody to ring. The Case moves to Review and its history shows the photos." |
| R851 | Same, items 11, 13, 27 and 30, four narrow cases built the simple way: a Send that carried only paperwork also counts for photos that arrive later without a second Send; a note can land on a Case archived in the same moment the settle runs; the bell can ring twice if the Worker stops between ringing and recording it; a photo still uploading when a link is cancelled can form a new batch that rings a second bell. Are they left as they stand? | Chosen: **"All four stand"**: "Each is recorded in FRD-28 as left as built, with your ruling." |
| R852 | Same, items 3, 5 and 37, already settled by R793, R791 and R805: a completed capture whose Case is later archived reads Expired; a record made by a photo link can later pair with a Case of a different Principal; the missing-item labels stay as listed. Do they stand? | Chosen: **"All three stand"**: "Recorded on #2026 as answered by R793, R791 and R805. Nothing changes." |
| R853 | Same, item 39: should the firm's form on a Quick link start with the customer's name and accident date staff typed on the Send page? | Chosen: **"Start with them"**: "The firm's form opens with the customer's name and accident date from the Send page already in it; the firm can change them." |
| R854 | Same, #2029: a photo link's photos took the order their uploads arrived in. Which order do they have before an engineer arranges them? | Chosen: **"Story order"**: "A photo link's photos that nobody has ordered yet follow the story. An order an engineer sets by hand is never changed, and other channels keep arrival order." (FRD-06, FRD-28) |
| R855 | Same, #2029: the link's message greeted "Mr Leroy Ejiofor" as "Hi Mr,". How is a name that starts with a title greeted? | Chosen: **"Skip a leading title"**: "A first word of Mr, Mrs, Miss, Ms, Mx or Dr is passed over, so the greeting is 'Hi Leroy,'. A name that is only a title and a surname ('Mr Ejiofor') is greeted 'Hi Mr Ejiofor,'." |
| R856 | Same, #2023 (R806 left these out): should Pegasus show the prototype's explanatory lines (the three page subtitles, the three hints on Send, the standing link's hint)? | Chosen: **"None"**: "As built: the pages carry no explanatory lines." |
| R857 | Same, #2023: should the Website link panel carry a line of its own? | Chosen: **"No line"**: "As built: the panel has its title and controls only." |
| R858 | Same, #2023: should the prototype's totals above Coming in ("Arrived today", "Awaiting instruction") and its "In progress" scope on Links we sent be added? | Chosen: **"Neither"**: "As built: the tab counts, status counts and the 'No case yet' filter already answer the same questions." |
| R859 | Same, #2023: should the prototype's third originals choice, "Required before sending", be built? | Chosen: **"No"**: "As built: originals follow in the background or are not sent. A customer on a slow connection is never held at Send." |
| R860 | Same, #2023: should a capture's row show "Engineer told at ..."? | Chosen: **"No"**: "As built: the Case's history already shows when the photos were in, and the Engineer's own notifications show the bell." |
| R861 | Same, #2023: should the Case's images gain the prototype's stage hints, its joined "Number plate, VIN and dashboard" group and an "Additional information" heading? | Chosen: **"None"**: "As built: each group has its own plain heading, and the two baskets are already told apart by what can go in the report." |
| R862 | Same, #2023: after a reopen the app's review strips show only this visit's pictures while the counts include earlier visits. Should the page say how many were sent earlier? | Chosen: **"Add a line"**: "Under a strip that has earlier photos: "Plus 8 sent earlier" (with the real number). A change to the app only." |
| R863 | Same, #2024: Resend or Remind on a link that has died refused with "The link has expired or was cancelled; extend it first.", and a cancelled link cannot be extended. What does it say? | Chosen: **"Two sentences"**: "Expired: "The link has expired; extend it first." Cancelled: "The link was cancelled; send a new one from the Case."" |
| R864 | Same, #2026 item 17: what words does a Case chosen by a member of staff with Match to a case show on Coming in? | Chosen: **"Matched by staff"**: "Reads the same to everyone and sits beside 'Matched automatically'." |
| R865 | Same, item 18: what does the chip on an inactive contact's standing link say, and which chip shows when the link is also paused? | Chosen: **"Contact inactive"**: "An amber chip reading 'Contact inactive', so staff see why the link does not work." When both are true, chosen **"Contact inactive"**: "Resuming the link would not make it work while the contact is inactive, so the chip names the thing that has to change first." |
| R866 | Same, items 19 and 38: the app says "photos" on the extras page and "images" on the review page, and "Registration plate" on the step label over a page headed "Number plate". Are they made the same? | Chosen: **"'photos' and 'Number plate'"**: "The review headings become 'Accident scene photos' and 'Other vehicle photos', and the step label becomes 'Number plate'." |
| R867 | Same, item 32: what do the Website link panel's pause buttons say? | Chosen: **"Pause the link / Resume the link"**: "On the Website link panel only. A contact's panel keeps 'Pause their link' and 'Resume their link'." |
| R868 | Same, item 20: six refusals on the staff pages had no sentence of their own. What are their sentences? | Chosen: **"Use these six"**, each beside its form. Website address of the wrong shape: "A website address is 6 to 60 lower-case letters, numbers and hyphens, and cannot start or end with a hyphen." Already taken: "That website address is already in use." Photo link settings with no lifetime ticked: "Offer at least one lifetime." Starting lifetime not ticked: "The lifetime links start on must be one of those offered." Saved address with no name or no address: "A saved address needs a name and an address." Quick sent without a Type: "Choose a Type for a Quick link." |
| R869 | Same, items 21 and 33: four labels were missing or borrowed. What are they? | Chosen: **"Use these four"**: the Website link's address box "Website address"; Retire, while it works, "Retiring…" (with the ellipsis character the other busy words use); the heading on Administration > Configuration "Photo links"; over the ticked lifetimes there "Lifetimes offered". "'Link lasts' stays over the starting lifetime, the same words as on the Send page." |
| R870 | Same, item 22: should a reminder show its sender in the link's history as Cancelled, Link extended and Call logged do (R805)? | Chosen: **"Reminded · name"**: "One line for each reminder in the same pattern as the other three, with the member of staff's name." |
| R871 | Same, item 24: should the Case history's reason for images completed by a photo link mention a cancelled link (R794)? | Chosen: **"Mention cancelled"**: "The photo link's photos are in: the sender pressed Send, or the link ran out or was cancelled with photos on it." |
| R872 | Same, item 34: should the hand-on screen still call the customer's link "a one-time link"? | Chosen: **"Drop 'one-time'"**: "We've created a link for AB12 CDE. Forward it to the customer and they'll be guided through the same photo steps." The rest of the paragraph is unchanged |
| R873 | Same, item 35: after a hand-on of a link sent from a Case the firm's phone was headed "Instruction received". What is the heading there? | Chosen: **"Sent to the customer"**: "For a link sent from a Case only; the paragraph below it is unchanged. A standing link keeps 'Instruction received'." |
| R874 | Same, item 36: a bodyshop's staff must send a VIN photo (R808) but were told "if it's easy to get to". What are they told? | Chosen: **"Drop 'if it's easy'"**: ""A clear shot of the VIN plate or chassis number." A customer still sees the optional wording, and a customer the link was handed on to is no longer asked for a VIN (R808)." |
| R875 | Same, item 41: when does the "Could you add a few more?" pop-up on the damage step appear? | Chosen: **"Only when an area is short"**: "It appears only when a damaged area has fewer than 3 photos, naming that area. The wording stays." |
| R876 | Same, item 31: is the first status "Sent, not opened" (the pages and the prototype) or "Sent not opened" (FRD-28's table)? | Chosen: **"'Sent, not opened'"**: "The pages stay as they are and FRD-28's table is corrected to match." |
| R877 | Same, item 25: two FRD-28 rules written during the readiness fixes cited no ruling. (a) Opened is recorded when the app first calls Pegasus, not when the page is fetched, because message previews fetch pages; a capture a firm starts on a standing link is Opened as it is made; a firm's phone that hands a link on never marks the customer's link opened. (b) Paperwork or video alone does not make a link read Photos coming in. Do both stand? | Chosen: **"Both stand"**: "Both are recorded as rulings and FRD-28 cites them. Nothing is rebuilt." |
| R878 | Same, item 23: should the app say why it will not send a file that is over the link's size limit? | Chosen: **"Show the same sentence"**: ""The file is larger than this link accepts." appears when the app refuses a file for its size." |
| R879 | Same: the layout fixes of #2029 can only be checked in a browser. How is the pull request checked before it is offered for landing (R695)? | Chosen: **"Serve the PR on the instance"**: "Once CI is green I point the test instance at that pull request's head, walk the lists at 760 px and the app in headless Chromium, save the screenshots, and return the instance to dev's tip. The walk adds a few test photos to Case OAK26003-p, as the last walk did." |
| R880 | Issue #2021 (10 November 2026): the issue says a group of mailed or uploaded images matching exactly one Case makes a Vehicle images record before merging, against FRD-19. Reading the code, that cannot happen: an e-mail's images file straight on the Case with no record, an uploaded group that matches a Case waits for staff, and a photo link's group never pairs in the same pass; the register-then-merge code is reached only by tests that build a mailbox group by hand, and FRD-19 still mentions an "eligible mailbox route" for groups that does not exist. What is the fix? | Chosen: **"Remove the unused route"**: "Delete the unreachable branch and the tests built on it, and correct FRD-19's sentence. Nothing staff see changes." |
| R881 | Issue #2020 (10 November 2026): R812 asks for the Photo capture lists' speed at today's count and at 2,000 captures of 30 photos each; there is no seeding tool and the test instance's data is not disposable (R696). Where does the 2,000 figure come from? | Chosen: **"A CI test"**: "A test creates 2,000 captures in CI's own database, loads both tabs and prints the timings, and stays as the before-and-after measure for the fix. Nothing is written to the test instance; the figure is from CI's hardware." |
| R882 | Same: the Photo capture page has no budget row of its own. Which budget does it have? | Chosen: **"500 ms"**: "It joins the Work Centre, Inbox list and Cases list as a hot list." The cut-off for finished links is decided once the number is in (R812) |
| R883 | Issue #1826 (10 November 2026): Graph names the mailbox in a notification as `Users/{user id}/Messages/{id}`, while desk@, info@ and engineers@ are subscribed by address because the application cannot read the directory, so the webhook's mailbox-prefix check never matched them and FRD-26 said "wrongly scoped notifications fail closed". How does the webhook match a notification to its mailbox: trust the subscription id, or resolve and store each mailbox's Graph user id (a directory grant and a column)? | Chosen: **"Trust subscription id (Recommended)"**: the subscription id Graph issued, the client state and the tenant, against the active subscription at the mailbox's current generation, identify the mailbox; the resource is read only for the message id. A wrongly scoped notification is one whose subscription is unknown, expired or of an older generation. FRD-26 "Mailbox wake-up and recovery" |
| R884 | Issue #1831 (10 November 2026): two e-mails of one conversation processed together; the second read its thread before the first's Case committed, so it stayed No Case Found and nothing looked at it again. A one-minute sweep would decide it again on the same four signals when its own mailbox conversation comes to hold a Case. How long after the e-mail may this happen (R189 and R377 already bound retroactive filing to two hours)? | Chosen: **"Within two hours (Recommended)"**: the same bounded window as R189/R377, counted from the e-mail's own processing; after it the mail stays No Case Found for a person. A message with any association row, a staff unlink's included, is never decided again this way. FRD-08 "Automatic association", FRD-22 |
| R885 | Issue #1982 (10 November 2026): the classifier agent deferred every Unclassified e-mail in a run once either lookup failed for any of them, while FRD-08 (R738) says an e-mail is left only when both lookups are unavailable; and it searches the old-system folders only when earlier mail names the firm's reference, so with the mailbox search down that search rarely runs. Which rule does the agent follow? | Chosen: **"Per e-mail, both (Recommended)"**: an e-mail is deferred only when both of its own lookups answered unavailable; when its mailbox search is unavailable the agent searches the folders with the reference or registration the e-mail names. FRD-08 "Mail categories and correction" |
| R887 | Issues #1496 and #1497 (10 November 2026): both rewrite the same three functions of the Case page script (`mount`, `settlePendingAnchor`, `jumpTo`) and the same contract test. One pull request for both, or two? | **"One PR, closes both (Recommended)"** |
| R888 | Issue #1496 (10 November 2026): where does a jump to a blocker's field finish? It put the section at the top and then glided to centre the field; for a field near the top of its section the centring pulled the page back into the section before, so the section row marked that one and not the one clicked. | **"Section top, centre only if deep (Recommended)"**: one scroll, made at once. The clicked section sits under the section row with the field outlined in view; the field is centred only when it lies more than half a screen down its section. The page never backs up into the previous section, so the section row always marks the section clicked. The glide goes, as a section link already jumps at once. FRD-16 amended |
| R889 | Issues #1496 and #1497 (10 November 2026): both ask for a real browser check, and CI has no browser lane; `scripts/Test-GlassBrowser.mjs` is the one precedent, run on demand. Where does the new check live? | **"On-demand script (Recommended)"**: `scripts/Test-CasePageBrowser.mjs`, the production script against scripted local answers in a headless browser, run before and after the fix with both results in the pull request, and one runbook entry. No CI lane |
| R890 | Issue #1814 (10 November 2026): reading the refusal "The report wording is unavailable. Refresh the Case and retry." showed a trap. After it the changed wording goes out with every later save from the page, and R572 refuses each save whole, so staff cannot save the field that would make the report ready (the salvage category, say) until they put the wording back by hand or refresh and lose it. How should #1814 go: hold it and file the trap, change the sentence and file the trap, or fix the trap? | **"Fix the trap in this run"** |
| R891 | Issue #1814 (10 November 2026): which rule removes the trap? The blocks left on screen after the refusal were drawn before the Case changed; sent again once the report is ready, a block the Engineer never touched would be compared with the sentence as composed now and stored as their edit, so a fix must not send those blocks again by itself. | **"Refuse once, then leave it out (Recommended)"**: R572 stands, and the save that carries the wording is refused whole. The page does not send that same refused wording again, so every later save lands, the salvage category included. The typed wording stays on screen and is sent again only if the Engineer changes it further; otherwise it goes when the Case is reloaded. FRD-11 amended |
| R892 | Issue #1814 (10 November 2026): which sentence replaces "The report wording is unavailable. Refresh the Case and retry."? Names R573, which left the wording of this message to its own issue. | **"Cause + what is kept"**: "The report is not ready, so its wording cannot be saved yet. Nothing in this save was stored. Your wording is still on the page." |
| R893 | Issue #1814 (10 November 2026): the sentence ends "Your wording is still on the page.", and the Automation wording tool (`pegasus_report_wording_save`) shows the same refusal to a caller with no page. What does the tool say? | **"First two sentences (Recommended)"**: the tool reads "The report is not ready, so its wording cannot be saved yet. Nothing in this save was stored."; the Case page adds "Your wording is still on the page." |
| R894 | Issues #2042 and #2043 (10 November 2026), both left out of the #1814 change and filed: a Report section kept on screen stores blocks the Engineer never touched as hand edits, and refused wording is not offered again once the report is ready. #2043 asked whether, once the report is ready, the page should offer the refused wording again so it saves without retyping. | **"resolve the issues too dont leave things out"**: both are resolved. Once a catch up shows the Case drawing its wording blocks again, the page sends the refused wording itself, once; refused again, it waits for the Engineer. It carries only what the Engineer wrote (#2042). Names R891, whose "otherwise it goes when the Case is reloaded" no longer applies once the report is ready. FRD-11 amended |
| R895 | Issue #1910 (10 November 2026): what may a name staff give a Send report attachment contain? Core required only `.pdf` with a non-blank stem; the browser's 120 covered the stem alone and `pegasus_report_send` had no bound, while policy names already make custody-invalid characters `_` and custody refuses names over 120 | **"Same as custody (Recommended)"**: each character no file name can hold (`" < > \| : * ? \ /` and control characters) becomes `_`, as policy names are made safe; the whole name, `.pdf` included, is at most 120 characters, the browser box dropping to 116; a longer name through MCP is refused with "A file name cannot exceed 120 characters." |
| R896 | Issue #1910 (10 November 2026): should a send whose attachments share a name (the report and the fee note both renamed `Report.pdf`) be refused? The recipient gets two files of one name and Graph draft reconciliation, matching by name and size, can fail on a retry | **"Add a number"**: Pegasus adds " (2)" to the second file's name instead of refusing; names compare case-ignored, " (3)" and on follow, the stem is cut to keep the name within 120, and a filed estimate keeps its filed name (R671), so the document carrying it is numbered |
| R897 | Issue #1912 (10 November 2026): R670 bounds "every staff-typed subject" at 500 while FRD-11 bounded whatever staff submit, so a prefilled "Re:" subject past 500 could never be sent and was refused as "Retry the operation"; a subject through `pegasus_report_send` could carry line breaks | **"Cap typed subjects only (Recommended)"**: a subject submitted unchanged from the derived one, or left blank, is sent as derived under the 998 ceiling; only a changed subject is held to 500, refused with "A subject cannot exceed 500 characters." on the dialog instead of "Retry"; line breaks in any subject become single spaces; FRD-11's sentence is reworded to match |
| R898 | Issues #1910–#1913 (10 November 2026): the scope of the Send report fix, with #1913 (the Original report card's guide word read from live valuation rows, not the frozen snapshot R666 names) needing the basis card's guide stored in each new generation's snapshot, which generations made before it lack | **"1910-1912 + 1913 (Recommended)"**: one PR; new generations freeze the basis card's guide; a generation made before it uses its snapshot's guide list when that list holds exactly one guide, else shows no brackets; #1923 stays with the operator |
| R899 | Test instance (10 November 2026): a claude.ai custom connector on the instance's Automation MCP reported "Couldn't reach" because the MCP was tailnet-only with no connector redirect (R679); either Funnel the whole 443 origin, behind password sign-in, with the claude.ai redirect configured (config only), or give the MCP its own Funnel origin on 8443 (a lifecycle change) | **"option A"**: Funnel on 443 exposes the whole origin and `AutomationMcp__RedirectUris=https://claude.ai/api/mcp/auth_callback` turns on the authorization-code flow; amends R679's "no connector redirect" and ADR-0088's "port 443 stays tailnet-only"; `tailscale funnel --https=443 off` undoes the exposure |
| R900 | The operator's request of 10 November 2026: "All valuations should not fetch a subsequent valuation when one has been fetched for the case already (essentially preventing someone from spamming the button and incurring costs)". Get valuation and `pegasus_valuation_get` asked the paid provider on every press; only the automatic Glass's request was asked once. Which sources, what scope, what counts as fetched, and what the card shows | **"Glass's, Brego, Cazana (Recommended)"** (AI market research unchanged); **"Once per source per month"**; **"Figures returned; again after reg/mileage change"**: only a provider answer with figures uses the ask, a failed or cannot-value press may be pressed again, and a corrected accepted registration or mileage allows each source and month one more; **"Button stays, refused on press"** with "{Source} has already been asked for this Case.". The automatic Glass's request is one such ask ([FRD-24](frd/frd-24-engineer-findings-damage-valuation-and-settlement.md#valuation-sources)) |
| R901 | Automation MCP wording (10 November 2026). The operator: **"AI seems reticent to use the take-over tool - does the schema or description for the tool dissuade usage of this?"** and, of `damage.impacts`, an AI's report that **"nothing in the connector says which way the car faces on that plan or where the nearside is"**, with **"when the AI tries to do the markers, they end up in random places (we usually mark around the edge of the vehicle)"** | Approved plan, wording only, no behaviour change: `pegasus_edit_begin`'s description and `takeOver` parameter say when to ask (a member of staff holds the Case) and that it is a request they can decline (R554 unchanged); a Case's lease-conflict refusal names `pegasus_edit_begin` with `takeOver` true; the server instructions' lease sentence gains "pegasus_edit_begin with takeOver asks the member of staff editing a Case to hand it over" (amending R526's approved copy). The `damage.impacts` format states the plan's orientation (front at the top, y 0; the vehicle's left, nearside, at x 0), that staff mark a row of small markers along the outline, and each drawing's landmarks; `pegasus_assessment_update`'s `fields` repeats the orientation. Markers off the outline are not refused |
| R902 | Delete case correspondence (9 November 2026): the operator asked for an optional tick, **Delete case correspondence**, on Send report, Send query reply and Send triage finding, removing the e-mails linked to the Case from the mailbox once the send has gone, so the Inbox holds only open work; FRD-21 and ADR-0071 said no other message is ever touched. What does "delete" do to the Outlook items? (The plan of that day numbered these four R767 to R770, which `dev` had issued by the time it was built.) | **"Move to Deleted Items"**: recoverable; Pegasus's copies, links and history untouched |
| R903 | Same, question 2: which of the Case's linked e-mails are moved? | **"Received e-mails only"**: Inbox-scope retained mail in an approved mailbox; Pegasus's own Sent items and uploaded `.eml` files are left |
| R904 | Same, question 3: the Inbox Reply / Reply all composer answers any message; where does it offer the tick? | **"Only on a query reply"**: exactly where Mark query done is offered |
| R905 | Same, question 4: should the Automation Actor's sends take it too? | **"Yes, add the parameter"**: `deleteCaseCorrespondence` on `pegasus_report_send` and on `pegasus_mail_send` for reply, reply_all and triage_reply |
| R910 | v50 design round, items A to E (10 November 2026; the operator's request "Workflow configuration page - this is becoming a dumping ground for new settings and needs refinement"): how should Workflow configuration be arranged and saved? | **"Approve all as drawn (Recommended)"** ([v50 round](https://github.com/collisionengineers/pegasus-design-archive/tree/design/v50-config-and-photo-capture/v50_planning)): one panel per concern, Required before Review, Case/PO, Chasing and due targets and Labour-rate cards, two columns from 1441px; each panel saves only its own settings with a Save in its head, under the version the page was drawn with; the chase interval and the five due targets one list of narrow boxes with "days" after each and no "(days)" in the labels; one Save for the labour-rate cards saves whichever card changed ([FRD-17](frd/frd-17-administration-workspace.md#workflow-configuration)) |
| R911 | v50 items F to H: where are the website link and Send a photo link's settings managed? | **"Approve all as drawn (Recommended)"**: a new Administration area **Photo links**, in the Configuration group after Workflow configuration and only while photo capture is on, holds the website link (from the Photo capture page, R797) and Send's starting values and lifetimes (from Workflow configuration, R813, R817), the latter under the heading **Send a photo link**; the website link's head holds Save for its defaults and More with Pause the link / Resume the link, Replace link (a dialog holding the Website address box) and Retire link, last ([FRD-17](frd/frd-17-administration-workspace.md#photo-links)) |
| R912 | v50 items I to M and P, with the operator's 10 November feedback "Theres a "more" button that opens into just one button? that seems redundant": how should the Photo capture lists read? | **"Approve all as drawn (Recommended)"**: one-line rows with long text ending in an ellipsis; each row leads with the act its state calls for, one other act is its own button and two or more go in More with Cancel link last (R795 stands); Sent by leaves Links we sent; Next reminder is blank when nothing is due; the list's heading and count are not repeated above the table; narrower windows drop columns rather than scroll (1480px, 1300px, 1180px); Log call from a row returns to the list ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#staff-pages)) |
| R913 | v50 items N and O: how should a photo link's page read? | **"Approve all as drawn (Recommended)"**: the status beside the registration; one primary, Resend when a reminder is due, Log call when the link needs a phone call, else Resend while it is live; View photos beside it; More, the size of its neighbours, with the other acts, Extend by 3, 7 or 14 days, and Cancel link last; the missing items as a short list with the photos and history in the main column and the facts and what they told us in a 360px aside ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#staff-pages)) |
| R914 | v50 item Q, from the operator's "resend and remind seem to do the same thing": both made a fresh link and sent the same e-mail (`PhotoLinkActions.ReissueAsync`); Remind only counted a reminder, and two reminders make Needs a phone call | **"Approve all as drawn (Recommended)"**: one act, **Resend**, which counts as a reminder only when one was due; Ask for it still always counts (R842). The Remind button and label go ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#statuses-and-reminders)) |
| R915 | The operator, 10 November 2026: "any email sending always needs a dialogue box - a button cannot just send an email on its own"; v50 item R, the composer's shape | The rule as written, and **"Approve all as drawn (Recommended)"** for the composer: a photo link's Resend (the list, the link's page and the Case's Resend the link) and Ask for it on a capture on a Case with an address open the Send chaser's composer, titled with the act: From, To (the link's address), Subject and Message in the standard wording with `{photo link}` filled on sending, "Edited for this one only." and Put the standard wording back; Cancel, Create link to copy, Send by e-mail; a sent message returns to where it was asked from with "Link sent to … by e-mail". Ask for it with nothing to e-mail still makes the link to copy. Supersedes R773's "Resend is one click" ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#statuses-and-reminders), [design](design/README.md#dialogs-and-feedback)) |
| R916 | PR #2052 review (10 November 2026), three defects: the Delete case correspondence tidy read a mailbox as writable while it was Approved, activated or not, so a ticked send on the test instance after a production copy would have moved mail in production mailboxes not enabled there (R627, R693); the Send report dialog kept the tick across Cancel and a later open, where FRD-11 and FRD-21 say it is unticked each time the form opens; and `docs/current-architecture.md` named the mover types the PR renamed | **"fix the three"**: the tidy moves only in a mailbox that is Approved and activated; each open of a dialog puts the tick back as the server drew it, unticked, or as posted on a refused send's redisplay (the PR's question on that redisplay stays open); the document names `IRetainedMessageMover` and `GraphRetainedMessageMover` |
| R917 | Same review: a staff forward held in instructions@ (R586, R651) is linked to its Case, and the original in the mailbox that forwarded it was moved only when it was itself linked (10 of the 11 forwards with a retained original on the instance's copy of production; the eleventh original held no Case and would have stayed in desk@) | **"in the case for instructions forwards we need that to get the original deleted too (even if there is only 1 email linked to the case, both must go)"**. As built: with a forward held in instructions@ that an approved mailbox sent goes the Inbox message of that mailbox which the forward's In-Reply-To and References name, linked to the Case or not; each of the 14 forwards on the instance carries one such identity, and it found the original wherever Pegasus had retained one. An original Pegasus never retained cannot be moved. An original that stands linked to another Case is left, the tidy never touching another Case's message (ADR-0071): the operator has not ruled on that case |
| R918 | The operator, 10 November 2026, on PR #2053: "implement sms sending of the links" (checking Microsoft Learn); which sender should Pegasus text from: an alphanumeric sender ID (one-way, 4–5 weeks' UK registration), a UK mobile number, or a short code? | **"UK mobile number"**: Pegasus texts a photo link from an SMS-enabled UK mobile number bought on its Azure Communication Services resource, to a UK mobile only, through the Web's managed identity; with no number configured nothing offers Send by text. Replies are not read. Supersedes R773's "text and WhatsApp are Copy link and the staff member's own phone" and "no provider" for texts, and narrows the PRD's BND-04 to exclude SMS other than the photo link ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#the-photo-link-text), [PRD](prd/pegasus-product.md#operating-and-commercial-constraints)) |
| R919 | What wording does the text use? | **"New short text template (Recommended)"**: a **Photo link (text)** template beside the Photo link e-mail's, with its placeholders, edited under Administration › E-mail templates; its built-in body is the agent's draft, because the operator asked: "make something reasonable and give it to me to sign off on". The draft awaits that sign-off ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#the-photo-link-text), [FRD-17](frd/frd-17-administration-workspace.md#e-mail-templates)) |
| R920 | Where can staff send by text? | **"Send page + Resend composer (Recommended)"**: wherever a photo link can be e-mailed, Send a photo link and the Resend / Ask for it composer offer **Send by text** to the mobile (the form's, or the link's own, which staff may change), through the composer as R915 requires; a texted Resend counts as a reminder as an e-mailed one does (R914) ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#the-photo-link-text)) |
| R921 | How much does Pegasus record of a text? | **"Accepted only; reports later (Recommended)"**: the link's history records "Sent by text · name" with the provider's message id when the provider accepts it, and "Text not sent · name" with its failure code when it does not; delivery reports through Event Grid are a follow-up issue, #2054 ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#the-photo-link-text)) |
| R922 | The operator, 10 November 2026: the Case page's Report section showed "Engineer's comments" twice, the recorded field and the Engineer's Comments wording block composed from it, and a block the Engineer reworded stopped carrying later field edits. Should anything change, and what happens to comments already recorded? | **"Remove and make it work like all the other sections, so its a tickbox enabled by default that shows what would be going in that sectio"**: the field `narrative.engineers_comments` is retired, the Engineer's Comments block composes the mileage sentence and, when unroadworthy, the unroadworthy sentence, and the Engineer writes their comments as the block's wording. Recorded comments: **"Drop them"**; the stored values are deleted, not carried into the block, and a frozen report keeps what it printed ([FRD-24](frd/frd-24-engineer-findings-damage-valuation-and-settlement.md), [FRD-11](frd/frd-11-reports-correspondence-and-reviewed-proposals.md#report-wording-blocks)) |
| R923 | The operator, 10 November 2026, on PR #2028's release-readiness review: production's App Service HTTP logs record every request path, so an opened photo link's `/c/<token>` would be kept in Log Analytics for 31 days against ADR-0090; turn them off, keep them and amend the ADR, or keep them unchanged? | **"Turn off the logs (Recommended)"**, confirmed when told that the per-handler p95 figures in operations were read from those unsampled logs: **"Off; use App Insights (Recommended)"**. The Web's diagnostic setting no longer sends `AppServiceHTTPLogs`; request timings come from Application Insights' sampled requests, as [engineering](engineering.md#page-budgets) already states ([ADR-0090](adr/0090-public-photo-capture-surface.md#decision)) |
| R924 | The release script refuses dev because merged PR #2027's rollup keeps a run cancelled one second after it started beside the green run that replaced it; fix the script, re-run the cancelled run, or both? | **"Fix the script (Recommended)"**: `Read-CandidateChecks` judges each workflow by its latest run on the pull request and each check by its latest attempt; a latest run that is not green still holds the candidate, so nothing is waived (R691) |
| R925 | How are the review's confirmed findings delivered? | **"Fix PR plus all items"**: one pull request to `dev` fixes every confirmed finding, the commit path's repeated reads, the part-time limits, the settle indexes and the receipt reconciliation included; the operator chose the index and read changes without the before-and-after figure CLAUDE.md's efficiency rule otherwise asks for |
| R929 | Eleven Not ready Cases on the test instance read "Review pending" (11 November 2026): each had its instructions and images, and was held by something the list row did not carry (no mileage, a failed document storage, an Audit without its original report). What should the Not ready chip show for such Cases? | **"Name the blocker (Recommended)"**: the Missing column uses words Pegasus already shows, **Document storage failed**, **Original report missing** and **Vehicle mileage** (joined to Images as "Vehicle mileage and images"), in that order of precedence; the Cases stay in Not ready as R759 rules; "Review pending" remains only for a Case staff reopened to Not ready with nothing missing. The quick detail's Current work and the Case's Next action name a failed document storage as the first requirement ([FRD-15](frd/frd-15-work-centre-queues-and-search.md)) |
| R930 | The operator, 11 November 2026: two PCH Audits (a.PCH26026-p and a.PCH26039-p) arrived with an original report that states the speedo reading (28,136 and 2,000 miles). Both vehicles are too new for MOT history, so the lookup found nothing and the Cases sit in Not ready with no mileage. FRD-23 ranks the supplied engineer report as the second mileage source, and the report reader already extracts the figure, but it is then dropped. Should a recognised original report's mileage fill an empty Case mileage? | **"Fill and move to Review (Recommended)"**: when the original report is recognised and the Case has no mileage, the report's figure is stored as the Case mileage, sourced to the report, and the Case moves to Review by itself, as a lookup does today; a mileage the Case already holds is never replaced ([FRD-23](frd/frd-23-case-draft-fields-provenance-and-global-checks.md), [FRD-13](frd/frd-13-case-lifecycle-and-workflow.md)) |
| R931 | Test instance, 11 November 2026: a.QDOS26232-p's John R Bell report is a scanned PDF, and reading it needs Azure Document Intelligence, which ADR-0088 and R677 keep as a production proof; the Worker refuses to start offline with the endpoint set, so the report's OCR job failed and it stayed filed as an instruction, as all 7 scans on the instance had. Should the test instance run OCR? | **"Yes, opt-in OCR (Recommended)"**: an opt-in setting for the instance, like its live DVLA, Box, Glass's and Graph ones, pointing at the production Document Intelligence resource; amends ADR-0088 and R677; scanned reports are then read and re-filed on the instance as in production. The Worker's `Features:LiveOcr` with `DocumentIntelligence:Endpoint` composes production's OCR adapter, and the instance authenticates with its existing Graph registration, `CollisionSpike Graph Intake`, which the operator approved together with one role assignment, `Cognitive Services User` on the production Document Intelligence resource `pegasus-prod-ocr-252ow37gij` for that registration, the write the plan approved on 11 November 2026 names ([ADR-0088](adr/0088-one-hosted-test-instance.md#decision)) |
| R932 | Three of those Cases could be recovered on the test instance with existing actions: retry document storage on QDOS26171-p (failed on the collision R719 fixed, never retried) and re-run the vehicle lookup on QDOS26198-p and QDOS26199-p (DVSA refused connections for 35 minutes on 9 November 2026). May the agent run them? | **"Yes, all three (Recommended)"**: run through the instance's own Automation actions on 11 November 2026; both lookups filled a mileage and the Cases moved to Review |
| R933 | The operator, 11 November 2026, after another Brego verify-browser e-mail from the test instance: production's Brego was healthy (every call 200, no device-approval call since 27 October), but the instance had no Brego session store, so after each Web start its first press signed in through device approval and e-mailed a code, and it had never produced a Brego valuation (#2062). How should the instance get a lasting Brego session? | **"Persist + copy prod cookie (Recommended)"**: save the session in the instance's own Azurite storage, the same store production uses, seeded once with production's existing 1-year cookie, so no new Brego session is created and no e-mail code is needed ([ADR-0084](adr/0084-brego-valuation-account-and-valuation-report.md)) |
| R934 | The operator, 11 November 2026, on QDOS26173-p: a ticked Other row in Valuation's Value increases unticked itself before a name could be typed, because the Save leaves out a blank Other row and the section was then redrawn from what was recorded; a name with no amount was recorded as a £0.00 increase and an amount with no name refused the save. When should an Other row count as a value increase? | **"Name and amount (Recommended)"**: an Other row counts once it is ticked and has both a name and an amount above zero. Until then nothing is saved for it, it stays on screen exactly as typed, and no save is refused because of it ([FRD-16](frd/frd-16-case-record-workspace.md#valuation)) |
| R935 | The same redraw wiped anything typed into a row that was not ticked. What should typing into an unticked Value increases row do? | **"Typing ticks the row (Recommended)"**: typing a name or an amount into an Other row, or changing a preset's amount, ticks that row. Unticking stays a deliberate click |
| R936 | With no guide card chosen the Save records no calculation, so every calculator control reverted about a second after it was set. What should the calculator do before a card is chosen? | **"Keep what was set (Recommended)"**: the controls stay as the Engineer set them and are applied as soon as a card is chosen. Nothing is recorded until then, as now |
| R937 | Three smaller defects stood in the same area: ticking Previous total loss stopped the Valuation section refreshing until the page was reloaded, ticking a blank Other row filled and saved Engineer's Value with no calculation recorded against it, and the two Other rows had identical spoken names. How are they delivered? | **"Everything in one PR"**: one pull request to `dev` fixes the Other rows and all three, the spoken names included |
| R938 | Both Other rows announced their tick box and their name box as "Other…". Which spoken names should the Other rows use? | **"Other increase 1 (Recommended)"**: tick box "Other increase 1", name box "Other increase 1 name", amount box "Other increase 1 amount", and the same for row 2. The visible "Other…" placeholder is unchanged |
| R939 | U76 on the test instance (11 November 2026): Parkhouse Assist's e-mail "Instructing repairs PHA 5028" became Unidentified on one picture, the full-page letterhead (1112x1414) laid behind the text from the header of its attached Word letter; the e-mail's signature banner had been dropped by R819, and the letterhead rule covered only a picture on every page of a PDF (R224). How should Pegasus stop this? | **"Header/footer rule (Recommended)"**: a picture placed in a Word document's header or footer is letterhead art, never a photograph; a picture in the body of a Word document is still judged by its size. Old receipts are not reprocessed, so U76 and the picture kept on it stay for staff (R820) |
| R940 | Same: an e-mail carrying a PDF is a document carrier and never Unidentified (R296, R297), but the rule named PDF only, so U76's e-mail, classified a new instruction and carrying a Word letter, still became Unidentified. Should an e-mail carrying a Word document be treated like one carrying a PDF? | **"Yes, same as PDF (Recommended)"**, then **"But also remember that we can receive documents that just contain images"** and **"e.g. saving a bunch of images as a pdf or .docx"**. Recorded as: a source carrying a Word document (.doc or .docx) is a document carrier, so with no Case the e-mail waits in No Case Found; the photographs inside the Word document stay vehicle images, are read for a registration on mail as a PDF's are (R472, R474) and are filed once the Case is matched. A Word document uploaded on its own is unchanged |
| R941 | The operator, 11 November 2026: **"Some mileage 'estimates' have been returning the last mileage from an MOT"**. With no clean interval in the MOT history (one MOT on record, two MOTs under 90 days apart, or an earlier day whose readings disagree) the estimator filled the latest reading unchanged, however old, as an Estimate, under R699's last clause; on the test instance 12 of 157 estimated Cases, the readings 13 to 324 days old. What should such a history give the Case? | **"Lifetime average"**: the last reading is projected forward at the vehicle's lifetime rate, the last reading divided by the days from first registration to that MOT; where no such date comes back the last reading stands as now. Approved with the plan the same day: the lifetime interval answers to the limits every interval does (under 90 days or over 300 miles a day is set aside and the reading stands), and it is not used after a corroborated odometer drop. This replaces R699's "with no clean interval the latest reading stands" (FRD-06) |
| R942 | Same: which DVSA date is the first registration, the plan having named `registrationDate` while DVSA describes `firstUsedDate` as "the date the vehicle was first registered" and `registrationDate` as "the date the vehicle was registered", the two differing for an import registered here after its odometer has run | **"First used, else registered"**: DVSA's `firstUsedDate`, and its `registrationDate` only where `firstUsedDate` is missing |
| R943 | Same: the Cases already holding a last MOT reading as their Estimated mileage, which a new lookup does not replace because a lookup never overwrites a mileage the Case has | **"Leave them"**: only new lookups follow R941; no stored Case mileage is changed |
| R944 | Same, a contradiction found on the way: FRD-06's mileage table gave an estimated mileage the report code `estimated` (R176), while the code resolves it to `online_data` and prints "The mileage has been calculated from online data."; no `estimated` sentence exists | **"Code is right"**: an estimate prints the online data sentence, and FRD-06's row is corrected to `online_data`; the Case page still reads Estimated (R176) |
| R945 | Issue #2073 (11 November 2026): on Search a long claim reference (`AFX/HALLIDAY/S519305.001`) is forced onto one line under the Case/PO and is drawn over Vehicle; no FRD or ruling says how it fits. How should it fit inside the Case/PO column? | **"Wrap (Recommended)"**: the claim reference continues on further lines inside the column and nothing is hidden; the Case/PO keeps its one line ([FRD-15](frd/frd-15-work-centre-queues-and-search.md#search)) |
| R946 | Same, found while measuring: the eight result columns are equal width, so the Case/PO itself runs into Vehicle on narrower screens (`a.QDOS26157-p` overlaps Vehicle's text at 1366 wide and `OAK26011-p` touches it at 1280), which the issue does not cover. What should happen to that? | **"Fix in this PR"**: the Case/PO column gets a set width that fits a reference, measured at 132px, and the other seven columns share the rest |
| R947 | Same, found while verifying R946: the 132px Case/PO column takes about 4px from each other column at 1580 wide, and the State chip "With Engineer", which already left its column (3px short of the Due text at 1580, 14px over it at 1366), then touches the Due text at 1580 and is 21px over it at 1366. How should the change handle that? | **"Size State too (Recommended)"**: State also gets a set width, 122px, which fits "With Engineer"; the other six columns share the rest, so more of their words wrap on narrower screens |
| R948 | The v51 round (Photo capture, Direct and Standing links, a link's page, Case images, Send a photo link and Vehicle images; [design/v51-photo-capture](https://github.com/collisionengineers/pegasus-design-archive/tree/design/v51-photo-capture/v51_planning)), drawn on the operator's 11 November requests "I think this is massively overcomplicated and far too much text … no way to even view the images", "we have standing links for places like a bodyshop or Accident management company, where they can basically send us anything, and then we have direct links for specific cases sent to a customer", "this "ask for it" button and even the general concept. What is this even meant to do? And "Match to a case"? We already have a system for that?": how is it settled? | **"You did not run stage 1, but v51 exists on the pegasus-design-archive repository on a seperate branch. Examine this carefully and integrate."**: items A to R as drawn, each variable at its default.
