Photo capture's tabs are **Direct links** (scopes Awaiting, Needs chase, Complete, Closed, All; one act per row, no Cancel link on rows) and **Standing links** (a directory any member of staff may copy a link from or show as a QR code, beside the submissions); **Ask for it** is retired and a standing or website capture is never sent again, reading **Uploading** until it settles and then **Received**; **Match to a case**, **No case yet**, **Matched automatically** and **Matched by staff** are retired, a submission's Destination being its Case or its Vehicle images record; a link's page shows its photos as thumbnails opening in the viewer, a one-line Missing bar, one Summary aside with short answers and folded History; the Case's Files → Images is one gallery with one photo link bar and the other links folded, and **Send photo link** in the Files head; Send and Resend show one channel at a time in a composer dialog; Vehicle images shows its Images first beside a Summary ([FRD-28](frd/frd-28-guided-photo-capture.md#staff-pages)) |
| R949 | The operator, 11 November 2026: **"we dont currently have the ability for viewing .doc or .docx files - plan out a full production ready integration for this"**. A browser cannot draw a Word file as it draws a PDF, so something must turn each one into a PDF the Case viewer then shows; neither host can run LibreOffice, and Microsoft Graph's conversion stops at 2 MB. Which converter? | **"Box converts"**: every held document is already in Box, and Box makes a PDF of `.doc` and `.docx` on request; Pegasus keeps the PDF in its existing rebuildable cache, and a file not yet held in Box has no View until it is. Checked first against real Word files in the test instance's Box subtree (three `.doc`, one `.docx`: a whole-document PDF of the stored version each time, at once where Box already held it and in about three seconds where it did not). The plan approved the same day: the existing View button opens the rendering in the existing viewer, Save as and the viewer's Download give the Word file through the audited download, a view writes no history row, and a file that cannot be shown says the existing "Preview unavailable" |
| R950 | Same: where a Word file gets View, the Case page's Documents list being the request, Triage Case drawing the same rows through the other viewer, and mail attachments before a Case having no in-app view even for PDF | **"Case page + Triage Case"**: both lists show the same Case documents through one route and one rule; mail attachments before a Case stay out |
| R951 | Same: when the PDF of a Word file is made, the budget for a slow render being 3 seconds behind a busy state (R458) | **"On first View, measure"**: the first View waits behind the busy state while it is made, and it is kept for later views; first-view time is measured on the test instance, and background preparation is added only if it is over the 3-second budget |
| R952 | The operator, 11 November 2026: **"Examine the case page and unroadworthy reasons. These need a control from the administration settings."** The wordings an Engineer saved from a Case were kept per Principal and offered only on that Principal's Cases, while the seven standard wordings were offered on every Case. One list offered on every Case, or a list per Principal? | **"One list for every Case (Recommended)"**: one bank for every Case, kept on Administration's Unroadworthy reasons page; wordings already saved under a Principal join it, a wording held under more than one Principal once ([FRD-24](frd/frd-24-engineer-findings-damage-valuation-and-settlement.md#settlement), [FRD-17](frd/frd-17-administration-workspace.md#unroadworthy-reasons)) |
| R953 | Same: the seven standard wordings were fixed in the code, so nobody could reword or remove them. Should the Administration page control those seven too? | **"Yes, they become ordinary rows (Recommended)"**: the seven are put into the bank once, as its first rows, and an Administrator rewords or removes them like any other wording; nothing stays fixed in the code |
| R954 | Same: should the Case page's "Save this wording to the bank" stay once Administration controls the bank? | **"Keep it (Recommended)"**: Engineers go on adding wordings from a Case, and an Administrator rewords or removes them afterwards |
| R955 | Same, the plan the operator approved on 11 November 2026: what Remove does, whether a change is logged, and the page's words | Approved as planned: Remove deletes the wording, and a reason already recorded on a Case keeps its own text; add, reword and remove write no Action log entry, as saving a wording from a Case writes none; the page is the Valuation presets table with the area name **Unroadworthy reasons**, the heading **Wordings**, the column **Wording**, and the sentences "No unroadworthy reason wordings are recorded.", "The wording was added.", "The wording was saved.", "The wording was removed.", "Enter a wording of up to 300 characters." and "The wording changed after this page was loaded. Reload it and try again." |
| R956 | Delete case correspondence, the review of #2052 on PR #2028 (11 November 2026; R945 to R955 are held by other open branches): R917 moved a staff forward's original only for a forward held in instructions@, and the test instance's staff forward into its own mailbox, pegasustest@ (R693), where the original was left | **"This essentially, when ticked, must delete all correspondence linked to the case (move to deleted folder). Notably, for anything forwarded to instructions/pegasustest it *must* also delete the original email (so two deletes even if only 1 email is linked)."** A forward held in instructions@ or pegasustest@ takes its original with it; a forward held in pegasustest@ is still answered as itself, R651 naming instructions@ alone |
| R957 | Same review, finding 2.2: a forward's reply headers name every earlier message of its thread, and the tidy moved every Inbox message they named in the forwarding mailbox unless it held another Case; all 20 staff forwards on the instance named one message. Which messages go with a forward? | **"Only the forwarded e-mail"**: one original for each forward, the message the forward was made from (its In-Reply-To, or the last of References where there is none); an earlier message of the thread goes only when it is itself linked to the Case |
| R958 | PR #2052's question 3, open since R917: a forward linked to one Case whose original in the forwarding mailbox stands linked to another Case | **"Leave it"**: the original stays as the other Case's open work and goes with a ticked send on that Case; ADR-0071 stands |
| R959 | Same review, finding 1, with PR #2052's question 1: both sent-mail tidies wrote their outcome to `CaseHistory`, which no page or tool reads, so the answered-instruction move has been unseen since ADR-0071. Where is a tidy's outcome shown, and in what words? | **"On the Notes tab, wording as above"**: on the Case's Notes tab, and the Triage Case's for a Reply with finding, with the sentences "The Case's received e-mails were moved to Deleted Items after the send: 2 moved, 1 already there, 0 no longer in the mailbox." and "1 of the Case's received e-mails could not be moved to Deleted Items (move_failed); attempt 1 of 3." as they stand. The labels, chosen as **"Proposed labels"**: "Case e-mails moved to Deleted Items", "Case e-mails not moved", "Instruction moved to Deleted Items", "Instruction not moved". Rows already written to `CaseHistory` are not carried over |
| R960 | Same review, finding 4, with PR #2052's question 2 (left open by R916): a refused Reply with finding came back ticked as posted, and the script ticked it again on a later open after staff had cleared it; Send report came back unticked and the Inbox reply composer as posted | **"Always come back unticked"**: after a refused send the tick is clear on all three surfaces, and staff tick it again. Supersedes R916's "or as posted on a refused send's redisplay" |
| R961 | Same review, finding 3.2: the answered-instruction tidy read a mailbox as writable while Approved, activated or not, so on the test instance after a production copy the instance's own report send answering a copied instruction would have moved it in a production mailbox not enabled there (R627, R693); R680 closed only the copied pending tidies | Approved with the plan of 11 November 2026: the answered-instruction tidy moves only in a mailbox that is Approved and activated, as R916 has the tick's tidy do. R680's closing of copied tidies stays |
| R962 | Same day: "all correspondence linked to the case" (R956) against R903's received e-mails only | **"R903 stands: received only"**: Pegasus's own Sent items and uploaded `.eml` files are left |
| R963 | Same review, findings 5 and 2.3: the Web reply composer sends and drops the tick where the query was resolved meanwhile, where MCP refuses; and a forward sent as an attachment carries no reply header, so its original is not found (none of the 20 forwards on the instance is one) | **"Record an issue"** for each: #2078 and #2079. No change now |
| R964 | Issue #1807 (11 November 2026): extracting still images from MP4 and MOV video evidence on Cases (`_CaseViewer.cshtml` plays confirmed videos on Standard and Audit Cases, and external AI tools can already file frames through `pegasus_document_add`, though `IAddCaseDocument` did not mark `ImagesComplete`). R916 stands (the forwarding mailbox must be Approved and activated, so a Disabled one is not written to), R958 stands (a retained original that holds another Case is left), and the mailbox's own Sent Items copy is left (R903). How far should the change go? | Chosen: **"Stop the script blanking; issue the rest"**: "\"Manual entry\" stops emptying the address: it puts the cursor in the address box with the text selected, ready to type over (as the labour-rate Custom option already does). What an emptied box should mean on every other field is a business rule; I record it as a GitHub issue with the evidence and build nothing for it here." Permission refusals and other errors still count towards three." ADR-0071 and FRD-21 change with the code under #2134 |
| R1069 | Same sweep, #2133 (part 1): a staff send (Inbox reply, Send report, Triage reply) that Graph answers with a server error or a timeout is recorded Unknown and staff land on the general error page; "The send result is not yet known." exists but shows only on a replay. What do staff see at that moment? | Chosen: **"Existing sentence, same page"**: "Staff stay on the page they sent from and see \"The send result is not yet known.\" with the send's status, on all three send routes. No new wording." Built under #2133 |
| R1070 | Same sweep, #2133 (part 2): an Unknown send becomes Sent only when the Sent poll finds it; no page settles, cancels, lists or counts Unknown sends. Should Pegasus do more with them? | Chosen: **"Nothing more"**: "An Unknown send stays Unknown until the Sent Items check finds it; staff send again if the recipient did not get it. FRD-21 says so and no screen is added." |
| R1071 | Same sweep, #2142: Report a problem files to GitHub (ADR-0055), whose organisation is suspended, so every send fails; R1059 left the destination undecided. Where do problem reports go? | Chosen: **"GitLab issues"**: "Each report becomes an issue in the pegasus project on GitLab with the problem-report label, as it did on GitHub. Needs a GitLab access token in Key Vault at the release. The staff sentence that says to check GitHub for the report ID would say GitLab." Built under #2142; follows R1059 |
| R1072 | Same sweep, #2078: in the Inbox reply composer, Mark query done and Delete case correspondence are dropped silently when the query was resolved between opening the form and Send; the Automation tool refuses the same send. What does the Web composer do? | Chosen: **"Send, drop the ticks"**: "As now: the reply goes, the ticks are ignored, and FRD-21 says so. The Automation tool keeps refusing, because a caller that names the tick should be told. No new wording." #2078 is closed ([FRD-21](frd/frd-21-outbound-correspondence-and-sent-evidence.md)) |
| R1073 | Same sweep, #2079 (left open by R963): a forward sent as an attachment carries no reply header, so Delete case correspondence moves the forward and leaves its original; none of the 39 staff forwards held on production and the test instance is one. Build for it or leave it? | Chosen: **"Leave them; close"**: "FRD-21 already says a forward sent as an attachment names no original. A ruling confirms that is accepted and #2079 closes. It can be raised again if staff start forwarding that way." |
| R1074 | Same sweep, #1920: the Inbox copy of a mailbox's own e-mail is dropped without trace because the Sent copy is the record (R582, R587), which loses the message on a mailbox that is Incoming only; no production mailbox is set so today. What should happen? | Chosen: **"Drop only if Sent is read"**: "The Inbox copy is dropped only when the mailbox is also Outgoing. An Incoming-only mailbox's own copy goes to intake like any other e-mail. Each drop writes one log line." Narrows R582 and R587; built under #1920 |
| R1075 | Same sweep, #2116: a report sending rule's "Instruction mentions" condition reads the instruction's subject and body only, though several Principals instruct by an attached PDF. Should it read the attachments' text? Then, reading the answer back: does "Instruction mentions" stop being a rule condition, a Repairer or Claim Source named in the instruction (subject, body or attachment text) filling that contact on a new Case instead? | **"\"Instruction Mentions\" - this doesnt store anything at present - the intended route on \"mentions\" is for this to be our logic on setting a repairer or claim source, which then triggers rules"**. Then chosen: **"Yes, retire the condition"**: "#2116 becomes: retire \"Instruction mentions\" as \"Images from\" and \"Bodyshop mentions\" were retired; an instruction that names a contact fills the Case's Repairer or Claim Source; PCH's rule keeps its reference-prefix condition. How RJS's \"Luton\" is restated, and the exact matching rule, come back to you from the investigation." Follows R1025 |
| R1076 | Same sweep, #1983: the classifier agent's `open_triage` accepts a registration found anywhere in the body, quoted thread history included (R732 says subject, body or attachment text). Does "body" mean the fresh text only? | Chosen: **"Fresh text only"**: "The agent's open_triage refuses a registration that appears only in the quoted history. Subject and attachment text are unchanged. FRD-08 says so." Built under #1983 |
| R1077 | Same sweep, #2108 (deferred by R1020): emptying a Case-page box whose value was extracted or looked up removes only staff's value, so the extracted value comes straight back. What does emptying a box record? | Chosen: **"Blank means blank"**: "Emptying a box records that staff cleared it: the box stays empty and the extracted value is no longer shown or used, though it stays in history. Staff retype a value to bring one back." Built under #2108 |
| R1078 | Same sweep, #2109 with #2108 and #2141: every Case save works the Inspection type out again from the address text, though FRD-06 and Core say it is stated by staff; a Case with no address shows "Not recorded" and is blocked. Is the type stated by staff or inferred? Then, reading the answer back: Image Based Assessment unless a place is set, or every Case starting as Image Based Assessment? | **"inspection type should always be set to image based assessment by default. the inspect AT is the variable option."** Then: **"Every cases inspection TYPE is an IBA - ALL cases. For every principle that is set to IBA by default, that also affects the inspect AT field and the inspection address (adds IBA automatically)"**. Recorded as its own issue for investigation; FRD-06 changes with the code |
| R1079 | Same sweep: FRD-06 says choosing Image Based Assessment by hand, or changing away from a filled one, needs a staff reason in Case history; the Case page asks for none. Should a reason be required? | Chosen: **"No reason"**: "Changing Inspect at is an ordinary edit, recorded in Case history like any other with who and when. FRD-06's reason sentence is removed. This is what the page does now." The sentence goes with the inspection-type change above |
| R1080 | Same sweep, #2109 (mileage unit): with a mileage and no unit the page reads "Not recorded" while the edit box shows Miles, and any save then records "miles" as staff's choice. What is an unstated unit? | Chosen: **"Miles is the default"**: "A mileage with no stated unit is in miles: the page shows Miles, and it is recorded as the default, not as a staff choice. Only a change to Kilometres is recorded as staff's. FRD-16 says so." Built under #2109 |
| R1081 | Same sweep, #2066: an instruction read on an accepted e-mail route creates its Case with no registration (R981); FRD-22 says the field is named as missing, but only "Vehicle mileage" is named and a mileage alone moves the Case to Review (R759). What should happen? | Chosen: **"Name it and hold"**: "The Not ready queue and the Next action name \"Vehicle registration\", and the Case stays Not ready until staff enter one, even if a mileage is recorded. R759's waiver gains this one exception." Built under #2066 |
| R1082 | Same sweep, #2065: FRD-15 says a failed Case storage is retried on the Case's Custody page, which has no retry; only the Automation tool's `retry_custody` re-arms it (R719). Who retries? | Chosen: **"Automation only"**: "No page change. FRD-15 is corrected to say a failed storage is retried by the Automation Actor (the R719 route) and #2065 closes." ([FRD-15](frd/frd-15-work-centre-queues-and-search.md)) |
| R1083 | Same sweep, #2032: a manual upload whose read is one character short of a matched Case's registration registers, after a decline or an expired wait, with the registration as read, where FRD-06 completes a short read from the Case. Which registration does it carry? | Chosen: **"As read, both times"**: "After a decline and after the wait runs out, the record carries the registration as read; staff correct it on the record. FRD-06 gains this exception for manual uploads. No code change." #2032 is closed ([FRD-06](frd/frd-06-vehicle-and-engineering-evidence.md)) |
| R1084 | Same sweep, #1907: the rule that a vehicle-capture document with no matching Case registers nothing (R662, R663) also stops a capture PDF that staff upload by hand, while a hand upload of several files can still register from plate reads. Is a hand upload covered? | Chosen: **"No: a hand upload registers"**: "R662 is about mail. A capture document staff choose to upload registers an Image Intake as it did before, alone or in a group, so staff have a record to link. FRD-02 says so." Built under #1907 |
| R1085 | Same sweep, #1909: each regeneration is the next Box version (R664) and Box keeps only the plan's limit, so opening an older generation whose version Box has dropped fails with a general error; the limit was recorded nowhere. What should Pegasus do, and what is the limit? | **"ours is currently set to 50. choose option 1 for handling this."** Option 1 was "Say so plainly": "Opening, resending or exporting that generation is refused with a sentence that says Box no longer holds that version; nothing else changes. The sentence comes back to you for wording. FRD-05 records the limit once known." The limit is in [FRD-05](frd/frd-05-documents-extraction-and-custody.md); the refusal is #1909's |
| R1086 | Same sweep, #1600 (R512's release blocker; the operator's later comment, "This is not a cutover issue - its a functionality issue"): a re-sent or forwarded instruction for a job that lives only in the old system creates a duplicate Case automatically. Check an old-system list, accept, or keep parked? | Chosen: **"Accept and close"**: "Old jobs are finishing and duplicates will die out. A duplicate that does appear is closed by staff under FRD-01's created-in-error route. #1600 closes and leaves the open-decisions register." Ends R512's hold on #1600 |
| R1087 | Same sweep, #1601 (R606, "Decide later"): each Principal's 2026 sequence started at 001 in Pegasus as it had in the old system; since R656 every Pegasus reference carries the temporary `-p` suffix. Is the suffix the answer? | Chosen: **"Yes, the suffix settles it"**: "No seeding. #1601 closes and leaves the open-decisions register, with a note that the -p switch stays on until numbers can no longer overlap (the 2027 sequence starts fresh)." |
| R1088 | Same sweep, #1775: since the compiled model went (R480, whose test was under 2 s per cold start) the Web app builds its model at each start; on B2, read from `AppServiceConsoleLogs` on 16 November 2026, 12 starts since 6 November gave a model build of 8.7 to 12.6 s on eleven and 17.3 s once (10.0 s at Release 4.0.0). Accept it or restore the compiled model? | Chosen: **"Accept about 10 s"**: "Starts happen only at deploys and restarts, when the site is briefly unavailable anyway. The figure is recorded against R480 and #1775 closes. Nothing is built." |
| R1089 | Same sweep, #1059 (part 1): of the 17 Report Sending SOP Principals, ALISON, AMS, KERR, KMR, SWAN and TEN now have a route and a profile and SWADE is retired (R126); ten are left. Rewrite the issue or close it for one issue per Principal as each writes in? | Chosen: **"Rewrite to the ten left"**: "#1059 is rewritten to today's state: SS, TP, WLS wait for sample instructions; HTU, MIDAS, MOTORX, WALKER wait for a sender address from you; it stays open as R131 says." |
| R1090 | Same sweep, #1059 (part 2): accidentspecialist.co.uk (ACSP) and claimsspecialists.co.uk (CS) are Claim Sources in RJS's rules and sixwaysclaims.co.uk (SIX) an intermediary for FW; each is a Principal contact and a Claim Source contact (R124). It is decided from the instruction itself, by the classifier or staff. The three leave #1059." |
| R1091 | Same sweep, #2061 (the operator's issue: rename Administration's E-mail templates to Templates): the scope its investigation recommends | Chosen: **"Yes, as described"**: the page is called Templates at `/Administration/Templates`, the saved notice is "Template saved." and the Action logs area reads "Template" (earlier log entries read so too); the row names stay ("Photo link", "Photo link (text)"); no redirect from the old address; nothing in the database is renamed. Built under #2061 |
| R1092 | Same sweep, #2129 question 1: the Unidentified tool dismisses any pending plate reading and the upload-group tool leaves any upload, where their pages offer each only in a narrower situation; the photo link tools already refuse what their page hides. Should these two refuse as well? | Chosen: **"Yes, refuse; one rule in Core"**: "The offer rules move into Core and the page, its own handler and the tool all follow them, as the photo link tools do. FRD-10 says so for all three." Built under #2130 |
| R1093 | Same sweep, #2129 question 2: a photo link's History lines and "Sent by" name only staff, so an act of the Automation Actor carries no name. What does the page show? | Chosen: **"Show \"Automation\""**: "History lines and \"Sent by\" read \"Automation\" for an act the Automation Actor made, the same word used elsewhere. No new wording." |
| R1094 | Same sweep, #2129 question 3: the page refuses to discard a manual upload without its consequences tick; the Automation tool's `discard` takes the call as the confirmation. Acceptable? | Chosen: **"Yes, the call confirms"**: "As built. FRD-10 records that the tool's discard needs no separate confirmation." ([FRD-10](frd/frd-10-mcp-automation-and-actor-boundary.md)) |
| R1095 | Same sweep, #2129 question 4, first put as "the Inspection report of a Case that has its Audit can never be generated, because a read-only Case is refused first" | **"check the code again on this - this doesnt seem accurate. verify and expand on the exact claim being made here, then put it back to me in another question(s)."** Re-read: the 1 October rule works while the Case is Not ready, Review, Report preparation or Report sent; only an older exception for a read-only Case is unreachable, in Held, Principal cancelled, Collision Engineers rejected, Created in error and Source e-mail unlinked, and its sentence "The case is read-only once Complete." names a state that no longer exists. Put again, chosen: **"No; delete the dead exception"**: "Nothing changes for staff. The unreachable exception and its stale sentence are removed from the page and the tools, and FRD-11 keeps the 1 October rule as it works today." |
| R1096 | Same sweep, #2129 question 5: on a change of Claim Source the Case page ignores the posted contact boxes (and ignores them when the Case has no source); the Automation tool applies a contact given with a newly chosen source and refuses one when the Case has none. Which is right? | Chosen: **"Each keeps its behaviour"**: "The page must ignore the boxes on a change, because they belong to the old source; a tool call that names a source and a contact together means that contact. FRD-10 and FRD-16 record why they differ. No code change." |
| R1097 | Same sweep, #2129 question 6: a stopped Automation client's 403 on a content route writes two security events (client disabled, access denied) where `/mcp` writes one. Keep both or write one? | Chosen: **"Write one"**: "A refused call from a stopped client writes the client-disabled event only, on every address." |
| R1098 | Same sweep, #2129 questions 7 and 8: wording only an AI client reads, shipped in 4.0.0 without the operator's word: "Photo capture is unavailable in this runtime.", "Sending by e-mail is unavailable in this runtime.", "Sending by text is unavailable in this runtime.", "<act> is not offered on this link; pegasus_photo_link_get lists the acts it offers.", "An optional mcp:-prefixed operation key labels the call's history."; and two groups of description cuts flagged on the old #2127 (ten light rewordings; the tool-level Delete case correspondence sentence on the report and mail send tools). The seven consent sentences are R1065's | Chosen: **"Accept all as shipped"**: "The five sentences stand and the cuts stay cut. Recorded as one ruling." |
| R1099 | Same sweep, #2129 question 9: the FRD-10 trim (R1042) removed a six-sentence paragraph on an unbuilt, externally scheduled network-drive scanning client; no other document mentions one. Restore it? | Chosen: **"Leave it out"**: "FRD-10 describes what exists. If a scanning client is wanted later it is raised as its own feature issue, and the old text is in git history." #2129 is closed on questions 1 to 9 |
| R1100 | Same sweep, #1847: production's database runs on the 7-day point-in-time default with no long-term retention, the storage accounts keep deleted blobs 7 days with no versioning, nothing is locked, none of it is declared in `infra/`, and the plan test pins the budget at GBP 75. What is declared? | Chosen: **"Declare today's, add locks"**: "The 7-day retention is written into the deployment files so it cannot drift, and delete locks (free) go on the database and both storage accounts. Budget stays GBP 75. A release that must delete a locked resource lifts the lock by name first." Built under #1847 |
| R1101 | Same sweep, #1732 (R506, deferred until a date is set): the recovery drill for OPS-09, a point-in-time restore of production to a scratch database, timed and then deleted. When? | Chosen: **"This week, on my word"**: "I prepare the exact commands and the scratch database's name and ask you for the go-ahead before any Azure write. The result is recorded in operations and the runbook gains the bring-into-service steps." Ends R506's deferral; the go-ahead itself is still to be given |
| R1102 | Same sweep, #1846: in the Exchange outage of 12 to 13 November 2026 the `instructions@` Inbox went unread for 9.5 hours and the one alert that fired went to `digital@`, a mailbox on the service that was out; a rule on failed `PendingWorkRecoveryFunction` runs (three or more in 15 minutes) would have caught it. Add it now, and who is the second recipient? | Chosen: **"Stale-Inbox rule + 2nd recipient"**: "Add that one rule and a second recipient outside Exchange. … The other gaps in #1846 stay listed there." The recipient, in the operator's words: **"alex.collisionengineers@gmail.com"**. Built under #1846 |
| R1103 | Same sweep, #1731 and #1863 (R505, deferred until a date is set): the staff UAT round and the rewrite of its 102-test pack, with production in daily use since 2 November 2026 | Chosen: **"Drop the UAT round"**: "Live use since 2 November stands as the acceptance. Both issues close, and the engineering document's evidence ladder is corrected to say so." The ladder names no UAT round ("Explicit acceptance by its designated authority"), so it stands as written and this row is that acceptance; the test-instance skill no longer plans a round. Replaces R505 |
| R1104 | Same sweep, #2086 and #2054: the photo-link text wording is signed off (R1001, R1002) and the UK SMS number is still not bought; delivery reports are deferred (R1004). Where does it stand? | Chosen: **"Still intend to buy it"**: "#2086 stays open, rewritten to what is left: buy the number, set it with a provision, one text reaches a phone. #2054 stays open and deferred behind it." |
| R1105 | Same sweep, #2009 (R1003, "Leave it for my phone pass"): the real-phone checks still owed (blur advice, a HEIC original from the camera roll, an Android recording's type, a video over 4 MB by Record on Android and by both routes on iPhone) | Chosen: **"Pass still to do"**: "#2009 stays open, rewritten as a short checklist of exactly what the pass still has to try." |
| R1106 | Same sweep, #2087, #2118 and #2000: the live checks owed after Releases 3.0.0 and 3.0.2 that were never recorded (Word view, Case videos, fee note VAT number, tidy timing, Query Store), and the page p95 after-reading for the slowness fixes; #2144 was closed the same way by R1065 | Chosen: **"Close two, keep the reading"**: "#2087 and #2118 close as confirmed by live use, as #2144 was. #2000 stays as an agent task: I take the p95 reading and record it in operations." |
| R1107 | #2136 (16 November 2026): a mailbox poll that is cut off part-way records no failure code. Core sees the Worker's 60 second budget and the Worker stopping as the same cancelled token. When the Worker itself is stopping and that cuts a poll off, should the mailbox also show a last error until its next good poll? | **"Any cut-off records"**: one rule: a poll cut off before it finished, by the 60 s budget, a Graph time-out or the Worker stopping, records a failure code and frees its lease at once; the next completed poll clears it |
| R1108 | Same: what Last error shows for a cut-off poll on Administration > Mailboxes and Service health | **"Reuse the general codes"**: `mailbox_poll_failure` for an Inbox and `sent_evidence_poll_failure` for Sent evidence, shown as any other unclassified failed poll is today; no new code and no new wording |
| R1109 | The test instance's No Case Found, 17 November 2026 (R1109 to R1111): 91 e-mails wait there. 60 were decided by the classifier on or before 12 November, under rules that R965 to R972 have since changed, and the classifier never re-reads an e-mail it decided itself (FRD-08), so they stay as decided: Fairway 31470 and Montreal Prestige RA6458998 refused a Case before R966, two Abrahams instructions before R969, chasers left as correspondence on R737's test that R968 replaced. What happens to the 60? A single re-review by the Automation actor and a standing rule that the classifier re-reads its own were offered | Chosen: **"Leave for staff"**: "Nothing is re-read. Staff clear the 60 by hand in the Inbox." |
| R1110 | Same: R1090 says mail from Accident Specialists, Claim Specialists and Six Ways is decided "from the instruction itself, by the classifier or staff", but R714 and R969 let the classifier create a Case only with the Principal Pegasus names, and Pegasus names none for a sender that is also a Claim Source for another Principal, so three Accident Specialists e-mails wait typed New instruction. May the classifier choose the Principal for these senders? | Chosen: **"Choose from the directory's (Recommended)"**: "For a sender the Contacts directory ties to more than one Principal (its own, and those it is a Claim Source for), the classifier may name one of exactly those when the instruction says whose work it is. Code checks the choice is in that set; otherwise the e-mail waits for staff. Amends R969." To build under #2149 |
| R1111 | Same: the instance's settings file carried neither `Box__OldSystemRootFolderId` (R970) nor `Features__LiveOcr` with its endpoint (R931), so all 22 old-system folder lookups the classifier made since 10 November answered unavailable, all 15 OCR jobs failed and 9 scanned e-mails could not be read. Add them and restart the run? | Chosen: **"Add both, restart (Recommended)"**: the two settings go in by the test-instance skill's procedure and the run restarts with its recorded run id; OCR spend as R931 allows |
| R1112 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026 (R1112 to R1121): six open issues have the Case page as their subject (#2108, #2109, #2110, #2111, #2146 with #2141). #1730 was left out because R502 says the `DetailsModel` split ships with the next design conversion, and #2133, #2066 and #2115 touch the Case page as one surface among several. Which of those should the run also take? | **"#2133 unknown send, #2066 no registration, #2115 list make/model, include 1730"**: all four are taken with the six. #1730 is built as its own change once the others have landed, which replaces R502's "With the next design conversion" |
| R1113 | Same, #2146 and #2141: KERR26002-p holds no inspection address (KERR is not an always-Image-Based-Assessment Principal; 43 of the test instance's 396 Cases are the same), so its Inspection type reads "Not recorded" and its report is blocked. Under R1078 the type reads Image Based Assessment. What should Inspect at hold on a Case where no place came with the instruction? | **"Inspect AT stays empty (unless there is logic that can fill it). Inspection type = always image based assessment"** ([FRD-06](frd/frd-06-vehicle-and-engineering-evidence.md#inspection-address)) |
| R1114 | Same: two things hang on an empty Inspect at, the Case starting Not ready naming Inspection address (R965) and the report being blocked. What should an empty Inspect at do? | Chosen: **"As now"**: "The Case starts Not ready naming Inspection address (R965) and the report is blocked until staff choose Inspect at. Only the \"Not recorded\" type and its own blocker go." |
| R1115 | Same: with the type always Image Based Assessment the report blocker can no longer be the "Inspection type" one, and the other reads "The vehicle is inspected at a physical location and no address is recorded for the report to print. Record the address on the Inspection details section." What does the blocker for an empty Inspect at say? | Chosen: **"Trimmed sentence"**: "Inspection address — No inspection address is recorded for the report to print. Choose Inspect at on the Inspection details section." |
| R1116 | Same: a Case inspected at a place prints "Vehicle located at:" and its address, with no Desktop Assessment section, and an Image Based Assessment Case prints those words and adds the section. With every Case's type Image Based Assessment, what does the report print when Inspect at is a place? | Chosen: **"Address + Desktop section"**: "Every report carries the Desktop Assessment section, as FRD-06 says every assessment is a desktop assessment. \"Vehicle located at:\" prints the place, or Image Based Assessment when that is Inspect at." |
| R1117 | Same: FRD-06 and FRD-16 say an Inspect at option whose value is not on the Case is "disabled, never shown empty"; the page leaves it out altogether (design round v36, item H). Which stands? | Chosen: **"The page: left out"**: "An option with no value on the Case is not listed. FRD-06 and FRD-16 are corrected to say so; no page change." |
| R1118 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026, #2111: a Case save refused for one field's value shows the general sentence "The case action was not applied because the case changed, edit mode was lost, or the action is not permitted." Core already has a sentence for some causes ("The vehicle registration can contain only letters, digits and spaces."); a value the server cannot read, such as 1e5 in Mileage, has none. What should the notice say? | Chosen: **"Core's sentence; \"<Field> is invalid.\""**: "Core's own sentence is shown where it has one, with the field's label in front when the sentence names no field (\"Vehicle registration: The value cannot exceed 20 characters.\"). An unreadable value reads \"Mileage is invalid.\", after the existing \"The mileage unit is invalid.\"" ([FRD-16](frd/frd-16-case-record-workspace.md)) |
| R1119 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026, #2109: R1080 settled that a mileage with no unit is in miles. One item it left: choosing Kilometres in the unit box before a mileage is typed is not kept (a unit is only saved with a mileage), and the box redraws on Miles. What should happen? | Chosen: **"Kept on the page"**: "Choosing a unit with no mileage saves nothing and the box stays on Kilometres, as Manual entry at Inspect at does (R1020); when a mileage is typed both are saved together. A reload before a mileage is typed returns to Miles." ([FRD-16](frd/frd-16-case-record-workspace.md#vehicle)) |
| R1120 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026, #2108 (R1077, "Blank means blank"): the Due by box on Case details shows staff's due date, or the instruction's inspection deadline until staff set one; emptying it brings the instruction's deadline back, and a test asserts that on purpose. Does "blank means blank" cover Due by? | Chosen: **"No, Due by is left as it is"**: "Due by is staff's override of the deadline, a different thing from an extracted value in a box: emptying it removes the override and the instruction's deadline shows again, as today. FRD-23's new clause says so." |
| R1121 | Same: R1077 says a cleared value is "no longer shown or used". Three places read what the instruction itself said, not the Case's current value, and already do so when staff correct a value: linking incoming mail to a Case by the instruction's registration, Recent cases search over the instruction's text, and the estimate document's fallback to the instruction's vehicle description when make and model are both empty. Do they stop using a value staff cleared? | Chosen: **"They honour the clear too"**: "Mail linking, Recent cases search and the estimate fallback also skip a value staff cleared. Three more readers change." ([FRD-23](frd/frd-23-case-draft-fields-provenance-and-global-checks.md)) |
| R1122 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026, #2133: on a Triage record an Unknown send blocks any further reply ("The existing correspondence operation must finish or be resolved before another action."), while R1070 says staff send again if the recipient did not get it. Should an Unknown send stop blocking a Triage reply, as it already does not block an Inbox reply? | Chosen: **"Stop blocking"**: "An Unknown send no longer blocks a further Triage reply, as on the Inbox reply, so staff can send again." ([FRD-21](frd/frd-21-outbound-correspondence-and-sent-evidence.md)) |
| R1123 | Same: the Case chaser, the photo link Send and Resend, and the Automation send tools use the same send engine and still reach the error page or a general failure when Graph's outcome is unknown; R1069 named three routes. Should these others be treated the same way? | Chosen: **"Build them in this run"**: the chaser, photo link Send and Resend and the Automation tools show the existing sentence with the send's status, or answer the Unknown operation, as the three ruled routes do |
| R1124 | Same: a report that goes stale in the instant before the send is refused by the send engine's last check, which has always recorded that refusal as Unknown; with R1069 built Send report says "The send result is not yet known." for it, though nothing was sent. What should happen? | Chosen: **"Record it Failed, separately"**: "A new issue: the engine records that refusal as Failed, so the page says the report was not sent. Until it is built the page says the result is not yet known, as the record does." |
| R1125 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026, #2110: a notice the script shows on a guide card ("cannot value", a refused answer, the needs-a-retail sentence, "the calculation could not be updated") disappears when the next save redraws the section; bringing it back can contradict the page if the card has meanwhile been filled or the save recorded the calculation. How long should such a notice stand? | Chosen: **"Until acted on, or superseded"**: "The notice survives a redraw and goes when the card's Get valuation is pressed again, a calculation succeeds or editing ends, and also when the redrawn card now holds figures or the save recorded the calculation, so it never contradicts the page." ([FRD-16](frd/frd-16-case-record-workspace.md)) |
| R1126 | Case page issue run, 17 November 2026, #2066 (R1081, "Name it and hold"): only the automatic e-mail route can create a Case with no registration, so holding every Not ready Case with none differs from holding automatic Cases alone only for a Not ready Case whose registration staff later clear; holding automatic Cases alone needs a stored marker of the route, a migration. Which Cases does the hold cover? | Chosen: **"Every Not ready Case"**: "Any Not ready Case with no registration is held and names it, as built. A Case already in Review or later is never sent back (R631, R759). No migration." ([FRD-13](frd/frd-13-case-lifecycle-and-workflow.md)) |
| R1127 | Same: when a Not ready Case has neither a registration nor a mileage, what is named? A mileage cannot be looked up without a registration | Chosen: **"Name both"**: "The queue names \"Vehicle registration and vehicle mileage\"; the Next action names the registration first, then the mileage once a registration is in. As built, and as R759 names the mileage on every route." |
| R1128 | Same: now that staff can send again after an Unknown send, "Latest send" on a Triage record and "Send status" on an Inbox message can keep showing the old Unknown after the second send is observed Sent, because the query that picks the latest send ranks unfinished sends, Unknown included, ahead of finished ones. Which send should those lines show? | Chosen: **"The newest send"**: "An Unknown send no longer outranks a later one: the line shows the most recent send, so a second send that reached Sent reads Sent." ([FRD-21](frd/frd-21-outbound-correspondence-and-sent-evidence.md)) |
| R1129 | #2130 and what lies beside it (17 November 2026): #2147 holds the three code follow-ups of the same Automation MCP review (R1093, R1095, R1097) and edits the same files; #1996, tool results that omit null properties, is a serialiser fault across every tool and is not ready for an agent. | R1130 | Same: R1092 makes a page's own handler and its tool refuse an act the page does not offer. What does an AI client read? | Chosen: **"Same pattern, with the flags"**: `pegasus_unidentified_get` gains `canDismiss` on each reading and `pegasus_upload_group_get` gains `canLeave`; the refusals read "dismiss_reading is not offered on this reading; pegasus_unidentified_get lists the readings that offer it." and "leave is not offered on this upload; pegasus_upload_group_get says whether it is." "FRD-10 records both." |

Stop removes the client's token permissions, so its token request is refused before Pegasus reads which client it is and writes `automation_token_rejected` alone; only the two content routes wrote two events | Put again with that, chosen: **"Leave it: one event, token-rejected"**: "The token address keeps writing automation_token_rejected alone for a stopped client, as it does today; no code changes there and the existing test stands." | R1138 | United Legal Assistance (ULA) and the garage authorisation workflow, 17 November 2026 (R1138 to R1152): a new Principal whose standalone Audits wait for a garage's estimate, an authorisation the Engineer gives in Audatex, and the repairer's invoice. | R1139 | Same: Which document types and operator-facing labels should Pegasus use for estimates and the repairer's invoice? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Split Estimate into 'CE estimate' and 'External estimate', and add 'Repair invoice' — existing Glass's files read as 'CE estimate' and earlier non-Glass 'Estimate' files become 'External estimate' (additive/forward-only migration)"** |
| R1140 | Same: How should Pegasus assign 'CE estimate', 'External estimate', and 'Repair invoice' when files arrive or are uploaded? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Auto-classify parsed estimates by header ('Collision Engineers' -> CE estimate, any other header -> External estimate; Glass's sessions -> CE estimate) on Repair Spec imports, untyped uploads, and emailed attachments, never overwriting an explicit staff-chosen type; Repair invoice (and non-parsed estimates) set by staff on Upload / Change type / MCP"** |
| R1141 | Same: How should the Stage 1–3 progression into Review and the R759 mileage gate work on Garage authorisation & invoice Audits? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Require a confirmed 'External estimate' alongside 'Images' and 'Vehicle mileage' to enter Review, and auto-fill an empty Case mileage and VIN from parsed Audatex estimates (never overwriting existing values)"** |
| R1142 | Same: How should Stage 4 ('Auth the garage') be modelled and completed by the Engineer? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Dedicated workflow milestone on the Case: Next action shows 'Authorise garage' with a one-click 'Mark done' button (no dialog or reason required), recording actor & timestamp in history, moving the Case to Not ready (keeping the assigned Engineer), with 'Undo garage authorisation' (with reason) in Actions"** |
| R1143 | Same: How should a Total loss (or Cash in lieu) outcome affect Stage 4 ('Authorise garage') and Stage 5 ('Repair invoice')? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Recording 'Total loss' (or 'Cash in lieu') as the Outcome waives both 'Authorise garage' and 'Repair invoice' (returning a post-auth Not ready Case to With Engineer) and omits the ULA Invoice; 'Repairable' and 'Contract repair' require both gates"** |
| R1144 | Same: How should chasing and the return to With Engineer work while the Case is in Not ready awaiting the Repair invoice? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Use the standard Not ready chase schedule (ChaseIntervalDays, default 7 days) with missing-material reason 'Awaiting repair invoice', and return automatically to With Engineer (keeping an enabled Engineer, else Unassigned) once a confirmed Repair invoice is filed"** |
| R1145 | Same: How should the ULA Principal Contact, reference format, and inbound e-mail route be configured? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Use '130c Lord Street, Southport, PR9 0AE' (phone '0330 999 9000', email 'claims@united-legal.co.uk'), standard 'a.ULA26001' reference format, seed ULA via idempotent migration, and accept the 'united-legal.co.uk' domain with a ULA instruction extraction profile classifying as standalone Audit"** |
| R1146 | Same: How should Pegasus handle the ULA Funding Matrix (trade rates) versus the retail Audit Report and ULA Invoice figures? | **"the funding matrix isnt that useful its just an extra doc we were given - dont put any functionality around it into pegasus"** |
| R1147 | Same: What file format and generation trigger should Pegasus use for the ULA Invoice? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Render the ULA Invoice as a PDF via QuestPDF (reproducing the exact layout, ULA logo, and footer banner from ULA_Invoice_257922_DRAFT.docx), generated automatically with 'Generate report' on Repairable / Contract repair outcomes under a Principal setting"** |
| R1148 | Same: How should the ULA Invoice's VAT number, payment terms, Invoice No / Ref fields, and default file name work? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Store 'Invoice VAT number' and 'Invoice payment terms' on Principal Report generation settings (omitting either row when blank); print the Claim reference (e.g. '257922') in both 'Invoice No' and 'Ref'; use 'Invoice Date' = Report date (dd/MM/yyyy); name the file 'ULA_Invoice_{ClaimRef}.pdf'"** |
| R1149 | Same: How should the ULA Invoice populate its addressee block (especially when the instruction carries no Claimant address) and Subject vehicle description? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Print Claimant name plus any recorded Claimant address lines (printing Claimant name alone when no address is recorded, matching R576 — never blocking report generation), and print Case Make + Model in uppercase in the Subject bar"** |
| R1150 | Same: How should the ULA Invoice calculate its line-item VAT, zero-value categories, and reconciliation with the Audit Report? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Follow the Current repair spec's VAT policy, reconcile any penny rounding delta onto the largest taxable row so Sub Total / VAT / Total always match the Audit Report to the penny, omit £0.00 rows (renumbered 1..N), and on Contract repair print one 'Contract Repair' row matching the agreed ContractSum"** |
| R1151 | Same: How should Stage 7 'Send report' attachments and final report-generation readiness work? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Add 'Offer external estimates (unticked)' and 'Offer repair invoices (unticked)' to Principal Report sending attachments (enabled on ULA), listing every confirmed current External estimate and Repair invoice on the Case as unticked checkboxes alongside pre-ticked Fee Note & ULA Invoice, and block final report generation until 'Authorise garage' and 'Repair invoice' are satisfied (unless Total loss / Cash in lieu)"** |
| R1152 | Same: Which seeded defaults should ULA start with? | Chosen: **"(Recommended) Seed ULA with £180.00 default fee, standard report file naming, and blank Invoice VAT number / Invoice payment terms / Notes on every Case (all editable in Administration > Contacts)"** |

Offered: (a) value today and fill the current month whatever month the card holds; (b) ask only for the current month | "Greyed out and guide month set to current month. Hovering this shows a tooltip saying CAP can only do valuations for the current day. Record a work item seperately for Cazana and Brego (also note for Brego I am fairly certain we CAN do later months for Brego (unclear on Cazana) - for this we will just improve the display for the guide value e.g. preventing a value greater than current date for those that cannot do future valuations. Your only scope is recording the work item on this." As built (the agent's reading): the CAP card's Guide month is read-only with that tooltip; it holds the current month, or a recorded card's own month until a figure is typed or fetched; a changed CAP card is recorded for the current month and one with no figures records nothing; every CAP ask is the current month's ask under R900. CAP figures can no longer be typed against an earlier month. The Cazana and Brego work item is #2161 |

| R1207 | CAP through Experian, 19 November 2026, after MR !61 was open: which Experian account each act uses | "this *should* actually function correctly but we now need an extra set of KV entries for experian. they have set up 2 accounts. one of these has CAP values permissions (merceraapi and merceraapiuat) and another (not within your scope) has the vehicle condition permissions. Therefore if you have set up the CAP to use merceraapi and merceraapiuat then this should function (we previously failed to use this account for condition checks, but it *should* have CAP)". As built (the agent's reading): `Experian:Account:*` and the four `experian-*` Key Vault entries are the account with CAP valuations, read back the same day as `merceraapi` in production and `merceraapiuat` on the test instance; CAP's Get valuation signs in with it. Each Experian act has a client and an account of its own, so the vehicle history check can be handed the second account without touching CAP; that account's Key Vault entries and settings are outside this change, and until they exist the history check still reads `Experian:Account:*` |
| R1211 | Release of 19 November 2026. `dev` already held two merges of the new Glass's work (!47, !55) beside work that is not Glass's, and eleven merge requests were open: six not Glass's (!53, !57, !58, !59, !61, !62) and five Glass's (!60, !64 to !67). What is released, and where each ends up | "You are merging all active MRs down, and performing a release. Any red CIs should be investigated and resolved." "All changes ASIDE from the new Glass's modifications (*all* of them - so the estimate building, estimate conversion, the live glass's estimate assistance from the automation etc) are in scope for this deployment." The end state asked for: "Zero active MRs. Main should contain all non-glass's related MRs currently outstanding, as well as any non-glass's relateds that are already part of dev. Dev should have all the Glass's MRs merged in." How it is reached is the lead's plan, approved by the operator the same day: the merges of !47 and !55 are reverted on `dev`, the six are combined on top and released as `dev`'s tip by the unchanged release script, and the Glass's work returns to `dev` after the release by reverting those reverts, followed by the open Glass's merge requests. The Parkhouse migration of !58 is renamed to sort before the three Glass's migrations, so the release that later carries the Glass's work still reads a new migration head |

Supersedes R1207's "until they exist the history check still reads `Experian:Account:*`"; the endpoints stay shared |
